## 1. Overall Structure of Motor Status Board and Register Spin Task

### `Spinning.vue` template layout

The view is a single-page console composed of:
1. `ConsoleHeader` — page title and status summary.
2. `MetricRow` — two `MetricCard`s showing selected motor and queue length.
3. A two-column `console-grid`:
   - Left: **Motor Status Board** (`MotorStatusBoard`)
   - Right: **Register Spin Task** (`ScheduleForm`)
4. A bottom full-width section: **Registration List** (`ScheduleQueue`)

```vue
<template>
    <section class="spinning-console">
        <ConsoleHeader
            eyebrow="Motor Control Console"
            title="Spinning Operations"
            ...
            :status-items="[{ label: 'Motors', value: motors.length }, { label: 'Scheduled Jobs', value: records.length }]"
        />

        <section class="metric-row">
            <MetricCard label="Selected Motor" :value="scheduleForm.motor_name || 'Not selected'" />
            <MetricCard label="Schedule Queue" :value="records.length" accent />
        </section>

        <div class="console-grid">
            <section class="panel-card">
                <PanelHeader kicker="Fleet" title="Motor Status Board" badge="Inventory" />
                <MotorStatusBoard :motors="motors" />
            </section>

            <section class="panel-card">
                <PanelHeader kicker="Scheduling" title="Register Spin Task" badge="Operator Entry" />
                <ScheduleForm
                    :motors="motors"
                    v-model="scheduleForm"
                    :errors="errors"
                    @submit="submitSchedule"
                />
            </section>
        </div>

        <div class="console-grid console-grid--bottom">
            <section class="panel-card">
                <PanelHeader kicker="Queue" title="Registration List" :badge="records.length + ' Item' + (records.length === 1 ? '' : 's')" />
                <ScheduleQueue
                    :records="records"
                    @cancel="cancelSchedule"
                    @delete="deleteSchedule"
                    @delete-selected="deleteSelected"
                    @clear-all="clearSchedules"
                />
            </section>
        </div>
    </section>
</template>
```

### `MotorStatusBoard.vue`

A read-only table with 6 columns: `ID`, `Name`, `Availability`, `Status`, `Target RPM`, `Actual RPM`.

```vue
<div v-if="motors.length" class="board-table">
    <div class="board-table__head">
        <span>ID</span><span>Name</span><span>Availability</span>
        <span>Status</span><span>Target RPM</span><span>Actual RPM</span>
    </div>
    <article class="board-row" v-for="motor in motors" :key="motor.id">
        <div class="board-cell board-cell--strong">{{ motor.id }}</div>
        <div class="board-cell">{{ motor.name }}</div>
        <div class="board-cell">
            <span class="availability-pill" :class="motor.avaliable ? 'availability-pill--good' : 'availability-pill--bad'">
                {{ motor.avaliable ? 'Available' : 'Unavailable' }}
            </span>
        </div>
        <div class="board-cell">
            <span class="status-pill" :class="`status-pill--${motor.status}`">
                {{ formatStatus(motor.status) }}
            </span>
        </div>
        <div class="board-cell">{{ motor.target_speed ?? 0 }}</div>
        <div class="board-cell">{{ motor.actual_speed ?? 0 }}</div>
    </article>
</div>
```

Status mapping inside `MotorStatusBoard`:

```js
formatStatus(status) {
    const map = {
        idle: 'Idle',
        running: 'Running',
        fault: 'Fault',
        offline: 'Offline'
    }
    return map[status] || status
}
```

### `ScheduleForm.vue`

A form with four fields: motor selection dropdown, scheduled datetime picker, speed input, duration input. It uses `v-model` with `modelValue`.

```vue
<div class="form-grid">
    <div class="field">
        <label class="label">Motor Selection</label>
        <select :value="modelValue.motor_name" @change="updateField('motor_name', $event.target.value)">
            <option v-for="motor in motors" :key="motor.id" :value="motor.name">
                {{ motor.name }}
            </option>
        </select>
    </div>

    <div class="field">
        <label class="label">Scheduled Time</label>
        <el-date-picker
            :model-value="modelValue.scheduled_time"
            type="datetime"
            value-format="YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss"
            format="YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss"
            @update:model-value="updateField('scheduled_time', $event)"
        />
        <button class="button is-light" @click="setImmediate">立即执行</button>
    </div>

    <div class="field">
        <label class="label">Spinning Speed</label>
        <input type="number" :value="modelValue.motor_speed" @input="updateField('motor_speed', Number($event.target.value))">
    </div>

    <div class="field">
        <label class="label">Spinning Time (s)</label>
        <input type="number" :value="modelValue.duration_sec" @input="updateField('duration_sec', Number($event.target.value))">
    </div>
</div>
```

## 2. How Device/ESP32 Data Is Fetched, Stored, and Bound

### Data flow

1. `Spinning.vue` polls the backend every **5 seconds** via `motorsApi.getList()` and `motorsApi.getRecords()`.
2. The backend (`get_motors` view) reads the `Motor` table, merges live device state from an in-memory cache (`_device_states` in `mqtt.py`), and returns a `motor_list`.
3. `Spinning.vue` stores the list in local `data.motors` and passes it down as props.

```js
// Spinning.vue
mounted() {
    this.getMotors()
    this.getRecords()
    this.refreshInterval = setInterval(() => {
        this.getMotors()
        this.getRecords()
    }, 5000)
},
beforeRouteLeave() {
    clearInterval(this.refreshInterval)
},
data() {
    return {
        motors: [],
        scheduleForm: { motor_name: '', scheduled_time: '', motor_speed: 0, duration_sec: 0 },
        records: [],
        errors: [],
        refreshInterval: null
    }
},
methods: {
    getMotors() {
        motorsApi.getList(this.$store.state.token)
            .then(response => {
                this.motors = response.data.motor_list
                if (this.motors.length && !this.scheduleForm.motor_name) {
                    this.scheduleForm.motor_name = this.motors[0].name
                }
            })
    },
    getRecords() {
        motorsApi.getRecords(this.$store.state.token)
            .then(response => {
                this.records = response.data.record_list
            })
    }
}
```

### Backend enrichment (`views.py::get_motors`)

```python
@api_view(['POST'])
def get_motors(request):
    if token_auth(request.data['token']):
        target_device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()
        live_states = get_device_states()
        state = live_states.get(target_device_id, {})
        device_online = state.get('is_online', False)

        motors = []
        for motor in Motor.objects.all().order_by('motor_index').values():
            motor_index = motor['motor_index']
            motor_telemetry = state.get('telemetry', {}).get(f'motor_{motor_index}', {})
            current_task = state.get('current_task', {})
            is_target_motor = current_task.get('motor') == motor_index

            target_speed = int(current_task.get('speed', 0)) if is_target_motor else 0
            actual_speed = int(motor_telemetry.get('rpm', 0))
            health_status = motor_telemetry.get('health_status', 'idle')

            if not device_online:
                motor['avaliable'] = False
                motor['status'] = 'offline'
            else:
                ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(target_device_id)
                motor['avaliable'] = ok
                motor['status'] = health_status if health_status in ('running', 'fault') else 'idle'

            motor['target_speed'] = target_speed
            motor['actual_speed'] = actual_speed
            motors.append(motor)
        return Response({'motor_list': motors}, status.HTTP_200_OK)
```

Key points:
- Data is **not** fetched via WebSocket in this view; it uses plain HTTP polling.
- The live state comes from the Django in-memory `_device_states` cache populated by the MQTT client in `mqtt.py`.
- There is **no Pinia store** used here; the project uses **Vuex 4** (`store/index.js`) only for auth token/email.
- Motor list is stored locally in `Spinning.vue` and passed as props.

---

## 3. How Motor Status Is Displayed

### Columns and binding

`MotorStatusBoard` receives `motors` prop and renders:
- `motor.id`
- `motor.name`
- `motor.avaliable` → pill `Available` / `Unavailable`
- `motor.status` → pill with class `status-pill--{status}`
- `motor.target_speed ?? 0`
- `motor.actual_speed ?? 0`

### Real-time updates

Updates are driven by the 5-second polling interval in `Spinning.vue`. The `MotorStatusBoard` itself has no internal reactive WebSocket subscription. Target/actual RPM values are computed on the backend from:
- `target_speed`: the speed of the device's `current_task` if it matches this motor index.
- `actual_speed`: the latest `rpm` telemetry for `motor_{index}`.
- `status`: `health_status` from telemetry (`running`/`fault`) or `idle`; overridden to `offline` if the device is offline.

### Health status logic (`mqtt.py::_update_motor_health`)

```python
def _update_motor_health(device_id, motor, rpm):
    ...
    is_target_motor = current_task.get('motor') == motor
    target_speed = int(current_task.get('speed', 0)) if is_target_motor else 0

    if is_target_motor and target_speed > 0:
        if rpm == 0:
            telemetry['zero_samples'] = telemetry.get('zero_samples', 0) + 1
        else:
            telemetry['zero_samples'] = 0

        if telemetry.get('zero_samples', 0) >= MOTOR_HEALTH_ZERO_THRESHOLD:
            telemetry['health_status'] = 'fault'
        else:
            telemetry['health_status'] = 'running'
    else:
        telemetry['zero_samples'] = 0
        telemetry['health_status'] = 'idle'
```

A motor is marked `fault` only after 3 consecutive zero-RPM samples while it has a non-zero target speed.

---

## 4. How Commands Are Sent to Motors

### Spin task command path

1. User submits form → `submitSchedule()` in `Spinning.vue`.
2. If a `scheduled_time` is provided, the frontend first calls `motorsApi.checkScheduleTime()` to compare it with server time.
3. The frontend adjusts the time if it is slightly in the past (`≤30s`) or prompts the user if it is more than 30s past.
4. Finally it calls `motorsApi.createSchedule(token, payload)`.
5. Backend stores a `Spinning` record with status `PENDING`.
6. `SpinningScheduler` (Django daemon thread, `scheduler.py`) polls every 0.5s and dispatches due tasks via MQTT.

### Frontend schedule submission (`Spinning.vue`)

```js
async submitSchedule() {
    this.errors = []
    let scheduledTime = this.scheduleForm.scheduled_time

    if (scheduledTime) {
        let checkResult
        try {
            const response = await motorsApi.checkScheduleTime(
                this.$store.state.token,
                scheduledTime
            )
            checkResult = response.data
        } catch (error) { ... }

        if (checkResult.expired) {
            if (checkResult.diff_seconds > 30) {
                // Prompt user to execute immediately
                try {
                    await ElMessageBox.confirm('预约时间已早于服务端当前时间超过 30 秒，是否立即执行？', ...)
                    scheduledTime = checkResult.server_now
                } catch { return }
            } else {
                // Auto-adjust to server_now
                ElMessage({ message: '...已自动调整为当前时间并立即执行', type: 'info' })
                scheduledTime = checkResult.server_now
            }
        }
    }

    const payload = {
        motor_name: this.scheduleForm.motor_name,
        scheduled_time: scheduledTime,
        motor_speed: Number(this.scheduleForm.motor_speed),
        duration_sec: Number(this.scheduleForm.duration_sec)
    }
    motorsApi.createSchedule(this.$store.state.token, payload)
        .then(() => { this.getRecords() })
        .catch(error => { ... })
}
```

### API service (`services/api/motors.js`)

```js
export default {
  getList(token) {
    return client.post('/api/get_motors/', { token })
  },
  getRecords(token) {
    return client.post('/api/spinning/', { token, data: null })
  },
  createSchedule(token, payload) {
    return client.post('/api/spinning/', { token, data: payload })
  },
  checkScheduleTime(token, scheduledTime) {
    return client.post('/api/spinning/check_time/', { token, scheduled_time: scheduledTime })
  },
  ...
}
```

### Backend dispatch

`views.py::spinning` stores the record:

```python
spin_instance['device_id'] = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()
spin_ser = SpinningSerializer(data=spin_instance)
if spin_ser.is_valid():
    spin_ser.save()
```

`scheduler.py` dispatches when due:

```python
result = dispatch_motor_task(
    device_id,
    motor.motor_index,
    task.motor_speed,
    task.duration_sec,
)
```

### MQTT payload format

`mqtt.py::dispatch_motor_task` publishes:

```python
topic = _device_control_topic(device_id)   # e.g. "esp32/7cdfa1e6d3cc/control" or "esp32_1/control"
cmd = f'cmd_{motor}_{speed}_{duration}'
client.publish(topic, cmd)
```

Example: `cmd_2_800_10` → motor index 2, 800 RPM, 10 seconds.

This matches the ESP32 firmware command format documented in the `AGENTS.md`.

---

## 5. Device Selection, Tabs, Pagination, Manual Refresh

### Device selection

- `ScheduleForm` has a **motor selection dropdown** (`motor_name`), not a device selection dropdown.
- The actual target **device** is resolved automatically on the backend by `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()`, which returns the first online & idle registered device, falling back to `settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID`.
- The `Spinning.vue` view has no concept of choosing which ESP32 to control.

### Tabs / pagination

- **No tabs** in `Spinning.vue`.
- **No pagination** in `MotorStatusBoard` or `ScheduleQueue`; both render the full array.
- The backend returns all motors and all records unsliced.

### Manual refresh

- There is **no explicit refresh button** in `Spinning.vue`.
- Polling is automatic every 5 seconds.
- The interval is cleared on `beforeRouteLeave`.

```js
mounted() {
    this.getMotors()
    this.getRecords()
    this.refreshInterval = setInterval(() => {
        this.getMotors()
        this.getRecords()
    }, 5000)
},
beforeRouteLeave() {
    clearInterval(this.refreshInterval)
}
```

Note: `beforeRouteLeave` is a Vue Router navigation guard; it is correctly used here to clean up the timer.

---

## 6. Related Stores, Composables, API Services, and Interfaces

### State management

- **Vuex 4** (`vue_frontend/src/store/index.js`), not Pinia.
- Store only holds auth state: `email`, `is_auth`, `token`.
- `Spinning.vue` accesses auth token via `this.$store.state.token`.

```js
// store/index.js
import { createStore } from "vuex";
export default createStore({
    state:{ email: '', is_auth: false, token: '' },
    mutations:{
        authSuccess(state){
            state.email = localStorage.getItem('email')
            state.is_auth = true
            state.token = localStorage.getItem('token')
        },
        authFail(state){ ... }
    }
})
```

### API services used by this view

- `motors.js` — all motor/schedule HTTP calls.
- `client.js` — axios instance with `baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8000'`.

```js
// services/api/client.js
import axios from 'axios'
const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8000',
  timeout: 10000,
  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
})
export default apiClient
```

### WebSocket service

- `websocket.js` exists and is used heavily by `Dashboard.vue`, but **it is NOT used by `Spinning.vue`**.
- `Spinning.vue` relies purely on HTTP polling.

### UI component imports in `Spinning.vue`

```js
import ConsoleHeader from '@/components/ui/ConsoleHeader.vue'
import MetricCard from '@/components/ui/MetricCard.vue'
import PanelHeader from '@/components/ui/PanelHeader.vue'
import MotorStatusBoard from '@/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue'
import ScheduleForm from '@/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue'
import ScheduleQueue from '@/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue'
```

### TypeScript interfaces

- The project is **JavaScript-only** (Vue 3 with Options API). No TypeScript interfaces or `.ts` files exist.
- Data shapes are implicit:
  - Motor object: `{ id, name, motor_index, avaliable, description, target_speed, actual_speed, status }`
  - Schedule form: `{ motor_name, scheduled_time, motor_speed, duration_sec }`
  - Record object: `{ id, motor_name, scheduled_time, motor_speed, duration_sec, status, ... }`

### Backend models involved

- `Motor` — static motor registry (`name`, `motor_index` 0-3, `avaliable`, `description`).
- `Spinning` — scheduled task records with statuses: `PENDING`, `SENT`, `RUNNING`, `FINISHED`, `FAILED`, `COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED`.
- `Device` — device registry with online state, task status, telemetry, current task.

### Tests

- `vue_frontend/src/__tests__/api/motors.spec.js` verifies the API wrappers.
- No component-level tests for `Spinning.vue`, `MotorStatusBoard.vue`, etc.

---

## Key Observations

1. **No WebSocket in Spinning view**: Real-time motor status is actually updated via 5s HTTP polling. The live WebSocket events used by `Dashboard.vue` are not subscribed to here.
2. **No device picker**: The target ESP32 is chosen automatically by the backend; the operator only picks a motor name.
3. **Server-time validation**: Recent commits added `check_schedule_time` to avoid clock-skew issues between operator PC and server.
4. **No pagination/tabs**: All motors and all records are rendered in full.
5. **No Pinia**: State management is Vuex 4, and only for auth.
6. **MQTT command format**: `cmd_<motor_index>_<speed_rpm>_<duration_sec>`, published to `esp32/<mac>/control` or `esp32_1/control`.

</details>

## 1. Device / ESP32 Model

**Model:** `main_page.models.Device`

```python
class Device(models.Model):
    device_id = models.CharField(max_length=32, unique=True, null=False,
                                 help_text='逻辑标识，如 esp32_1')
    client_id = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True,
                                 help_text='MQTT Client ID，如 ESP32S3_xxx')
    mac_address = models.CharField(max_length=17, blank=True,
                                   help_text='硬件 MAC 地址，后续多设备区分用')
    label = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True,
                             help_text='用户自定义别名，如 "反应釜 A"')
    is_registered = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    is_online = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    last_heartbeat = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    task_status = models.CharField(max_length=16,
                                   choices=DEVICE_STATUS_CHOICES,
                                   default='idle')
    current_task = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    telemetry = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, null=False)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True, null=False)

    class Meta:
        db_table = 'main_page_device'
        ordering = ['device_id']
```

Key fields:
- `device_id`: logical identifier used everywhere for routing (e.g. `esp32_1`, `esp32_7cdfa1e6d3cc`).
- `mac_address`: stored for hardware distinction, but MQTT topics are derived from `device_id`.
- `client_id`: optional MQTT client id (e.g. `ESP32S3_7cdfa1e6d3cc`).
- `task_status` / `current_task` / `telemetry`: runtime snapshots kept in DB and in memory (`_device_states`).

---

**Model:** `main_page.models.Motor`

```python
class Motor(models.Model):
    id = models.AutoField(primary_key=True, null=False)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=64, null=False)
    motor_index = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(
        default=0,
        validators=[MinValueValidator(0), MaxValueValidator(3)],
        help_text='对应 ESP32 的电机索引 0-3'
    )
    avaliable = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=256)
```

Migration `0031` ensures four motors named `Motor 0` … `Motor 3` with `motor_index` 0..3.

## 4. Motor Command Publishing

### Primary dispatcher (`main_page/mqtt.py`)

```python
def dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration):
    ok, reason = can_dispatch_to_device(device_id)
    if not ok:
        return {'success': False, 'error': reason}
    if client is None or not client.is_connected():
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'MQTT client unavailable'}

    topic = _device_control_topic(device_id)
    cmd = f'cmd_{motor}_{speed}_{duration}'
    ...
    _set_device_task_status(device_id, 'busy', current_task={...})
    info = client.publish(topic, cmd)
    return {'success': True, 'topic': topic, 'command': cmd, 'rc': info.rc}
```

### Topic mapping (`main_page/mqtt.py`)

```python
def _device_control_topic(device_id):
    if not device_id:
        return None
    if device_id.startswith('esp32_') and len(device_id) == 6 + 12:
        return f"esp32/{device_id[6:]}/control"
    # 兼容旧 topic
    return f"{device_id}/control"
```

Examples:
- `esp32_1` → `esp32_1/control`
- `esp32_7cdfa1e6d3cc` → `esp32/7cdfa1e6d3cc/control`

### REST view wrapper (`main_page/views.py`)

```python
@api_view(['POST'])
def device_dispatch_task(request):
    device_id = request.data.get('device_id')
    motor = int(request.data.get('motor', 0))
    speed = int(request.data.get('speed', 0))
    duration = int(request.data.get('duration', 0))

    if not device_id:
        return Response({'detail': 'device_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

    result = dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration)
    ...
```

---

## 5. How the Backend Distinguishes Devices When Forwarding Commands

1. **Logical id:** every command is addressed by `device_id` (the unique string in the `Device` table).
2. **Topic derivation:** `_device_control_topic(device_id)` derives the MQTT control topic from the id. If the id contains a 12-hex-char MAC suffix, it uses the new layered topic `esp32/<mac>/control`; otherwise it falls back to `<device_id>/control`.
3. **MAC storage:** `Device.mac_address` is kept for admin/display purposes but is **not** directly used to build topics; the MAC is reconstructed from `device_id` (`_device_id_to_mac`).
4. **Auto-selection:** `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred=None)` returns the first registered device that is online and idle, or falls back to `settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID`.

```python
def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None):
    if preferred_device_id:
        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)
        if ok:
            return preferred_device_id

    Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')
    for device in Device.objects.all().order_by('device_id'):
        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(device.device_id)
        if ok:
            return device.device_id

    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')
```

The scheduler uses this to pick a target when a `Spinning` record is due:

```python
device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id)
result = dispatch_motor_task(
    device_id,
    motor.motor_index,
    task.motor_speed,
    task.duration_sec,
)
```

---

## 6. WebSocket Endpoint

`django_backend/django_backend/routing.py`:

```python
websocket_urlpatterns = [
    re_path(r'websocket/$', consumers.MyConsumer.as_asgi()),
]
```

Actions handled in `MyConsumer.receive`:

```python
action == 'emergency_stop'
action == 'resume_device'
action == 'dispatch_task'      # calls dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration)
action == 'acknowledge_device'
action == 'get_snapshot'       # broadcasts current device states
```

The consumer also emits:
- `device_snapshot`
- `mqtt_connection_status`
- `telemetry` / `task_status` / `device_status` / `device_reply` broadcasts from `mqtt.py`.

---

## 8. Other Relevant Models

- `Spinning` — scheduled motor jobs with lifecycle (`PENDING`, `SENT`, `RUNNING`, `FINISHED`, `FAILED`, `COMPLETED`, `CANCELLED`), `device_id`, `motor_name`, `motor_speed`, `duration_sec`.
- `MotorEvent` / `MotorData` — legacy event + telemetry storage keyed by `device_id`.
- `BatchJob` / `BatchStepExecution` / `RecipeStep` — recipe-based workflow; motor steps resolve to `cmd_<motor>_<speed>_<duration>`.
- `CommandOutbox` — tracks every outgoing MQTT command with status (`QUEUED`, `SENT`, `ACKED`, `FAILED`).
- `TelemetryIngest` — stores JSON device replies.

---

This should give you the full picture of how the Django backend models devices/motors, exposes them via REST/WebSocket, and forwards commands to MQTT.

    99	    methods: {

   101	            motorsApi
   102	                .getList(this.$store.state.token)
   103	                .then(response => {
   104	                    this.motors = response.data.motor_list

   115	                })
   116	        },
   117	        async submitSchedule() {
   118	            this.errors = []
   119	            let scheduledTime = this.scheduleForm.scheduled_time
   120	
   121	            // 以服务端当前时间为基准，根据偏差决定行为
   122	            if (scheduledTime) {
   123	                let checkResult
   124	                try {
   125	                    const response = await motorsApi.checkScheduleTime(
   126	                        this.$store.state.token,
   127	                        scheduledTime
   128	                    )
   129	                    checkResult = response.data
   130	                } catch (error) {
   131	                    this.handleApiError(error)
   132	                    return
   133	                }
   134	
   135	                if (checkResult.expired) {
   136	                    if (checkResult.diff_seconds > 30) {
   137	                        // 超过 30 秒：询问用户是否立即执行
   138	                        try {
   139	                            await ElMessageBox.confirm(
   140	                                '预约时间已早于服务端当前时间超过 30 秒，是否立即执行？',
   141	                                '时间过期',
   142	                                {
   143	                                    confirmButtonText: '立即执行',

   145	                                    type: 'warning',
   146	                                    closeOnClickModal: false
   147	                                }
   148	                            )
   149	                            // 用户选择“立即执行”
   150	                            scheduledTime = checkResult.server_now
   151	                        } catch {
   152	                            // 用户选择“重新调整时间”：取消发送，不执行任何操作
   153	                            return
   154	                        }
   155	                    } else {
   156	                        // 30 秒以内：自动调整为当前时间并立即执行
   157	                        ElMessage({

   159	                            type: 'info'
   160	                        })
   161	                        scheduledTime = checkResult.server_now
   162	                    }
   163	                }
   164	            }

   166	            const payload = {
   167	                motor_name: this.scheduleForm.motor_name,
   168	                scheduled_time: scheduledTime,
   169	                motor_speed: Number(this.scheduleForm.motor_speed),
   170	                duration_sec: Number(this.scheduleForm.duration_sec)
   171	            }
   172	            motorsApi

   192	        },

   217	                })
   218	                .catch(this.handleApiError)

   130	@api_view(['POST'])
   131	def get_motors(request):
   132	    if token_auth(request.data['token']):
   133	        target_device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()
   134	        live_states = get_device_states()
   135	        state = live_states.get(target_device_id, {})
   136	        device_online = state.get('is_online', False)
   137	
   138	        motors = []
   139	        for motor in Motor.objects.all().order_by('motor_index').values():
   140	            motor_index = motor['motor_index']
   141	            motor_telemetry = state.get('telemetry', {}).get(f'motor_{motor_index}', {})
   142	            current_task = state.get('current_task', {})
   143	            is_target_motor = current_task.get('motor') == motor_index
   144	
   145	            target_speed = int(current_task.get('speed', 0)) if is_target_motor else 0
   146	            actual_speed = int(motor_telemetry.get('rpm', 0))
   147	            health_status = motor_telemetry.get('health_status', 'idle')
   148	
   149	            if not device_online:
   150	                motor['avaliable'] = False
   151	                motor['status'] = 'offline'
   152	            else:
   153	                ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(target_device_id)
   154	                motor['avaliable'] = ok
   155	                motor['status'] = health_status if health_status in ('running', 'fault') else 'idle'
   156	
   157	            motor['target_speed'] = target_speed
   158	            motor['actual_speed'] = actual_speed
   159	            motors.append(motor)
   160	        return Response({'motor_list': motors}, status.HTTP_200_OK)
   161	    return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
   162

   164	@api_view(['POST'])
   165	def spinning(request):
   166	    if token_auth(request.data['token']):
   167	        if request.data.get('data'):
   168	            spin_instance = request.data['data']
   169	            print(spin_instance)
   170	            if spin_instance.get('motor_name'):
   171	                # 前端无时区字符串按 Django 配置的本地时区解析
   172	                naive_time = datetime.strptime(
   173	                    spin_instance['scheduled_time'],
   174	                    '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S',
   175	                )
   176	                scheduled_time = naive_time.replace(
   177	                    tzinfo=ZoneInfo(settings.TIME_ZONE)
   178	                )
   179	                # 过去时间保护：若早于当前时间，则设为立即执行
   180	                if scheduled_time < timezone.now():
   181	                    scheduled_time = timezone.now()
   182	                spin_instance['scheduled_time'] = scheduled_time
   183	                # 自动选择当前可下发的实际设备（优先在线的 ESP32 MAC 设备）
   184	                spin_instance['device_id'] = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()
   185	                spin_ser = SpinningSerializer(data=spin_instance)
   186	                if spin_ser.is_valid():
   187	                    spin_ser.save()
   188	                    return Response(status.HTTP_200_OK)
   189	                return Response(spin_ser.errors, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   190	            return Response(status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   191	        else:
   192	            records = []
   193	            for record in Spinning.objects.all().order_by('-scheduled_time').values():
   194	                record['scheduled_time'] = timezone.localtime(record['scheduled_time'])
   195	                if record.get('dispatched_at'):
   196	                    record['dispatched_at'] = timezone.localtime(record['dispatched_at'])
   197	                if record.get('completed_at'):
   198	                    record['completed_at'] = timezone.localtime(record['completed_at'])
   199	                records.append(record)
   200	            return Response({'record_list': records}, status.HTTP_200_OK)

   203	@api_view(['POST'])
   204	def spinning_cancel(request, job_id=None):
   205	    """取消状态为 PENDING 的预约任务。"""
   206	    if not token_auth(request.data.get('token')):
   207	        return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
   208	
   209	    record_id = job_id or request.data.get('id')
   210	    if not record_id:
   211	        return Response({'detail': 'id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   212	
   213	    try:
   214	        spinning_record = Spinning.objects.get(id=record_id)
   215	    except Spinning.DoesNotExist:
   216	        return Response({'detail': 'Record not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)
   217	
   218	    if spinning_record.status != 'PENDING':
   219	        return Response(
   220	            {'detail': f'Cannot cancel task with status {spinning_record.status}.'},
   221	            status=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
   222	        )
   223	
   224	    spinning_record.status = 'CANCELLED'
   225	    spinning_record.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
   226	    serializer = SpinningSerializer(spinning_record)
   227	    return Response(serializer.data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
   228

   230	@api_view(['POST'])
   231	def spinning_delete(request):
   232	    """根据 id 列表删除预约记录。"""
   233	    if not token_auth(request.data.get('token')):
   234	        return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
   235	
   236	    ids = request.data.get('ids')
   237	    if not ids or not isinstance(ids, list):
   238	        return Response({'detail': 'ids list is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   239	
   240	    cleaned_ids = [i for i in ids if isinstance(i, int)]
   241	    if not cleaned_ids:
   242	        return Response({'detail': 'No valid ids provided.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   243	
   244	    deleted_count, _ = Spinning.objects.filter(id__in=cleaned_ids).delete()
   245	    return Response({'deleted': deleted_count}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

   258	@api_view(['POST'])
   259	def check_schedule_time(request):
   260	    """校验前端所选预约时间与服务端当前时间的偏差。"""
   261	    if not token_auth(request.data.get('token')):
   262	        return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
   263	
   264	    scheduled_time_str = request.data.get('scheduled_time')
   265	    if not scheduled_time_str:
   266	        return Response({'detail': 'scheduled_time is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   267	
   268	    try:
   269	        naive_time = datetime.strptime(scheduled_time_str, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')
   270	    except ValueError:
   271	        return Response({'detail': 'Invalid scheduled_time format.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
   272	
   273	    scheduled_time = naive_time.replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(settings.TIME_ZONE))
   274	    server_now = timezone.now()
   275	    diff_seconds = (server_now - scheduled_time).total_seconds()
   276	
   277	    return Response({
   278	        'server_now': server_now.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S'),
   279	        'diff_seconds': diff_seconds,
   280	        'expired': diff_seconds > 0,
   281	    })

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me formulate the clarifying questions. The key ambiguities are:

1. **Motor-to-Device relationship**: Currently the `Motor` model has no `device_id` field. All 4 motors are shared globally and status is resolved against one dispatchable device. For multi-device support, we need to know if each device has its own 4 motors, or if motors are still global but status is fetched per-device. The user mentioned "除 MAC 格式的设备 ID 不同其他基本一致的 ESP32S3" - so each ESP32 has 4 motors. So we likely need to fetch motor status per device.

2. **Backend API changes**: Does the user expect us to modify backend `get_motors` to accept `device_id`, or is there already an endpoint? Currently `get_motors` auto-resolves.      1	import logging

     5	from django.db import close_old_connections
     6	from django.utils import timezone

     8	from .models import Motor, Spinning
     9	from .mqtt import dispatch_motor_task, resolve_dispatchable_device_id
    10	
    11	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12	
    13	POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 0.5
    14	
    15	
    16	class SpinningScheduler:
    17	    """Django 进程内守护线程：轮询 Spinning 表，到期后下发电机任务。"""
    18	
    19	    _instance_lock = threading.Lock()
    20	    _thread = None
    21	    _stop_event = threading.Event()
    22	
    23	    @classmethod
    24	    def start(cls):
    25	        """启动调度线程；重复调用不会创建多个线程。"""
    26	        with cls._instance_lock:
    27	            if cls._thread is not None and cls._thread.is_alive():
    28	                logger.info('SpinningScheduler already running')
    29	                return
    30	            cls._stop_event.clear()
    31	            cls._thread = threading.Thread(
    32	                target=cls._run,
    33	                daemon=True,
    34	                name='SpinningScheduler',
    35	            )
    36	            cls._thread.start()
    37	            print('SpinningScheduler started')
    38	
    39	    @classmethod
    40	    def stop(cls):
    41	        """停止调度线程。"""
    42	        cls._stop_event.set()
    43	        if cls._thread is not None:
    44	            cls._thread.join(timeout=2)
    45	
    46	    @classmethod
    47	    def _run(cls):
    48	        while not cls._stop_event.is_set():
    49	            close_old_connections()
    50	            try:
    51	                cls._process_due_tasks()
    52	            except Exception as exc:
    53	                logger.exception('SpinningScheduler error: %s', exc)
    54	            time.sleep(POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS)
    55	
    56	    @classmethod
    57	    def _process_due_tasks(cls):
    58	        """处理已到期的 PENDING 任务。"""
    59	        now = timezone.now()
    60	        pending = Spinning.objects.filter(
    61	            status='PENDING',
    62	            scheduled_time__lte=now,
    63	        ).order_by('scheduled_time')
    64	
    65	        for task in pending:

    68	    @classmethod
    69	    def _dispatch_task(cls, task):
    70	        """尝试下发单条任务，并在数据库中记录结果。"""
    71	        # 先原子地将状态从 PENDING 改为 SENDING，防止并发重复触发
    72	        claimed = Spinning.objects.filter(
    73	            id=task.id,
    74	            status='PENDING',
    75	        ).update(
    76	            status='SENDING',
    77	            updated_at=timezone.now(),

    80	            return
    81	
    82	        task = Spinning.objects.get(id=task.id)
    83	
    84	        try:
    85	            motor = Motor.objects.filter(name=task.motor_name).first()
    86	            if motor is None:
    87	                raise ValueError(f'Motor "{task.motor_name}" not found')
    88	
    89	            device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id)
    90	
    91	            result = dispatch_motor_task(
    92	                device_id,
    93	                motor.motor_index,
    94	                task.motor_speed,
    95	                task.duration_sec,
    96	            )
    97	
    98	            if result.get('success'):
    99	                Spinning.objects.filter(id=task.id).update(
   100	                    status='SENT',
   101	                    dispatched_at=timezone.now(),
   102	                    error_message='',
   103	                    updated_at=timezone.now(),
   104	                )
   105	                print(f"Dispatched scheduled task {task.id}: {result.get('command')}")
   106	            else:
   107	                raise RuntimeError(
   108	                    result.get('error', 'Unknown dispatch error')
   109	                )
   110	        except Exception as exc:
   111	            error_text = str(exc)[:256]
   112	            Spinning.objects.filter(id=task.id).update(
   113	                status='FAILED',
   114	                error_message=error_text,
   115	                updated_at=timezone.now(),
   116	            )
   117	            print(f"Failed to dispatch scheduled task {task.id}: {exc}")

  "content": "# Plan: Multi-Device Motor Status Board & Task Registration\n\n## Date\n2026-07-16\n\n## Scope\nModify the `Spinning.vue` dashboard so that the **Motor Status Board** and **Register Spin Task** panels support multiple ESP32S3 devices distinguished by MAC-format device IDs.\n\n## Requirements Confirmed with User\n1. **Each Device has its own 4 motors (0-3).** The global `Motor` table remains the 4-motor template; live telemetry is fetched per `device_id`.\n2. **Register Spin Task** must explicitly select a **Device** first, then allow multi-select of that device's motors, and dispatch to the selected device.\n3. **Manual refresh** button coexists with the existing 5-second auto-polling. Clicking refresh immediately fetches the currently selected device's motor status.\n4. **Multi-motor scheduling** stores a **single `Spinning` record with a motor list**; the scheduler dispatches commands to each selected motor.\n5. **Device tabs** in Motor Status Board are labeled by **MAC address** (e.g. `7c:df:a1:e6:d3:cc`).\n\n## Key Findings from Codebase\n- `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue` currently polls `/api/get_motors/` and `/api/spinning/` every 5s and passes `motors` to `MotorStatusBoard` and `ScheduleForm`.\n- `MotorStatusBoard.vue` renders a plain 6-column table (`ID`, `Name`, `Availability`, `Status`, `Target RPM`, `Actual RPM`).\n- `ScheduleForm.vue` has a single motor dropdown, datetime, speed, and duration.\n- Backend `get_motors` (`django_backend/main_page/views.py`) auto-resolves a single dispatchable device via `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` and returns only that device's 4 motors.\n- Backend `spinning` view auto-fills `device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()`; frontend cannot choose the target device.\n- `Spinning` model has a single `motor_name` field and a `device_id` field.\n- `SpinningScheduler` (`scheduler.py`) dispatches one motor per record.\n- Device registry and live state already exist (`Device` model, `/api/device_list/`, `_device_states` in `mqtt.py`).\n- Git root: `E:/Platform_G2`. Current uncommitted change: `django_backend/db.sqlite3` (must be excluded from commit).\n\n## Implementation Approach\n\n### Backend Changes\n\n#### 1. `django_backend/main_page/models.py`\n- Add a JSONField `motor_names` to `Spinning` to store the list of selected motors for multi-motor tasks.\n- Keep `motor_name` for backward compatibility with existing records (legacy single motor).\n- Add a helper property `effective_motor_names()` that returns `motor_names` if present, otherwise `[motor_name]`.\n\n#### 2. `django_backend/main_page/views.py`\n- Modify `get_motors(request)`:\n  - Accept `request.data.get('device_id')`.\n  - If provided and the device exists in `_device_states` or `Device` registry, use it as `target_device_id`.\n  - Otherwise fall back to `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` for backward compatibility.\n  - Return an extra field `device_id` in the response so the frontend knows which device the data belongs to.\n- Modify `spinning(request)`:\n  - Accept `device_id` from `request.data['data']`.\n  - Accept `motor_names` list from payload.\n  - Validate that the device exists; if not, fall back to `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()`.\n  - Validate that all `motor_names` exist in the `Motor` table.\n  - Store `device_id` and `motor_names` in the `Spinning` record.\n  - For backward compatibility, also set `motor_name = motor_names[0]`.\n- Add a small utility `format_mac(device_id_or_mac)` used to render MAC labels consistently.\n\n#### 3. `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py`\n- Modify `_dispatch_task(task)`:\n  - Use `task.effective_motor_names()`.\n  - Resolve device once via `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id)`.\n  - Loop over motors; for each, call `dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor.motor_index, task.motor_speed, task.duration_sec)`.\n  - Mark record `SENT` only if all succeed; mark `FAILED` if any dispatch fails (record the first error).\n\n#### 4. `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py`\n- Update `SpinningSerializer` so that `motor_names` is writable and `motor_name` remains optional for new records.\n\n#### 5. Database Migration\n- Create an auto-migration for the new `Spinning.motor_names` JSONField:\n  ```bash\n  cd /e/Platform_G2/django_backend\n  python manage.py makemigrations main_page\n  ```\n\n### Frontend Changes\n\n#### 1. `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js`\n- Change `getList(token)` to `getList(token, deviceId = null)` and include `device_id` in the POST body when provided.\n- Change `createSchedule(token, payload)` payload shape to include `device_id` and `motor_names` instead of only `motor_name`.\n- Add `getDeviceMotors(token, deviceId)` convenience wrapper if desired.\n\n#### 2. `vue_frontend/src/services/api/devices.js`\n- Ensure `getList()` already calls `/api/device_list/` (it does). No changes needed unless token auth is required; current endpoint is `GET` and unauthenticated. If the project requires auth, add token header. Based on current code, keep it as-is.\n\n#### 3. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue`\n- Add props: `devices` (Array), `selectedDeviceId` (String), `loading` (Boolean).\n- Render an Excel-like tab bar at the bottom (or top) of the panel:\n  - Each tab label = formatted MAC address of the device.\n  - Active tab highlighted.\n  - Clicking a tab emits `select-device`.\n- Add a **Manual Refresh** button in the panel header area; clicking emits `refresh`.\n- Keep the existing 6-column table but render it for the currently selected device only.\n- Show device-level summary above the table (online/offline, task status).\n\n#### 4. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue`\n- Add props: `devices` (Array), `motors` (Array — still used as motor template).\n- Change `modelValue` structure to:\n  ```js\n  {\n    device_id: '',\n    motor_names: [], // multi-select\n    scheduled_time: '',\n    motor_speed: 0,\n    duration_sec: 0\n  }\n  ```\n- Add a **Device** dropdown; when changed, reset `motor_names`.\n- Replace single **Motor Selection** dropdown with a multi-select checklist (e.g. `select multiple` or Element Plus `el-select` with `multiple`). Options are the global 4 motors; label can include device context.\n- Keep speed, duration, scheduled time, and immediate button.\n- Emit `submit` on button click.\n\n#### 5. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue`\n- Update the table to display `Device` (MAC/label) and `Motors` (joined list) columns.\n- Backward compatibility: if a legacy record has only `motor_name`, display that.\n\n#### 6. `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue`\n- Add local state:\n  - `devices: []`\n  - `selectedDeviceId: ''`\n  - `deviceMotors: {}` — cache of `{ [deviceId]: motor_list }`\n  - `loadingStatus: false`\n- Fetch device list on mount via `devicesApi.getList()`.\n- After device list loads, default `selectedDeviceId` to the first online device or first device.\n- Modify `getMotors()` to call `motorsApi.getList(token, selectedDeviceId)` and store result in `deviceMotors[selectedDeviceId]`.\n- Pass `filteredMotors = deviceMotors[selectedDeviceId] || []` to `MotorStatusBoard` and `ScheduleForm`.\n- Keep 5s polling for records and selected-device motors.\n- Handle `select-device` event from `MotorStatusBoard` to switch `selectedDeviceId` and immediately fetch motors.\n- Handle `refresh` event to immediately fetch selected device motors.\n- When submitting schedule, pass `device_id` and `motor_names` in payload.\n- Update `ConsoleHeader` status items to show device count and motor count.\n\n## File Changes Summary\n\n### Backend\n- `django_backend/main_page/models.py` — add `motor_names` JSONField and helper property.\n- `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py` — expose `motor_names`.\n- `django_backend/main_page/views.py` — `get_motors` accepts `device_id`; `spinning` accepts `device_id` + `motor_names`.\n- `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py` — dispatch to multiple motors in one record.\n- New migration file in `django_backend/main_page/migrations/` for `Spinning.motor_names`.\n\n### Frontend\n- `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js` — support `device_id` and new payload shape.\n- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue` — device tabs + manual refresh.\n- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue` — device + multi-motor selection.\n- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue` — show device and motors list.\n- `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue` — orchestrate device selection and per-device data.\n- `vue_frontend/src/__tests__/api/motors.spec.js` — update tests for new `getList` and `createSchedule` signatures.\n\n## Testing Plan\n1. Run backend migration and start Django.\n2. Create/register at least two `Device` records with different MAC addresses.\n3. Open `Spinning.vue`:\n   - Verify device tabs appear with MAC labels.\n   - Switch tabs; verify `get_motors` request includes `device_id`.\n   - Click **Manual Refresh**; verify immediate request and table update.\n4. Register a multi-motor task:\n   - Select a device, select Motors 0 and 2, set speed/duration, submit.\n   - Verify `/api/spinning/` payload contains `device_id` and `motor_names: ['Motor 0', 'Motor 2']`.\n   - Verify `SpinningScheduler` dispatches two commands.\n5. Verify legacy records with single `motor_name` still display and dispatch correctly.\n6. Run frontend unit tests: `cd vue_frontend && npm test` (or vitest) and update assertions.\n\n## Git Commit Instructions\n- Commit from `E:/Platform_G2`.\n- **Exclude** `django_backend/db.sqlite3`.\n- Suggested commit message:\n  ```\n  feat(spinning): multi-device motor status board and task registration\n\n  - Motor Status Board now groups motors by Device with MAC-labeled tabs\n  - Add manual refresh button to fetch selected device's live motor status\n  - Register Spin Task supports explicit Device selection and multi-motor dispatch\n  - Backend get_motors and spinning endpoints accept device_id and motor_names\n  - SpinningScheduler dispatches commands to each motor in a single record\n  ```\n\n## Rollback Notes\n- Migration adds a nullable JSONField with default `list`; safe to reverse.\n- Legacy `motor_name` is kept populated, so old records continue to work even if `motor_names` is empty.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Multi-Device Motor Status Board & Task Registration

## Date
2026-07-16

## Scope
Modify the `Spinning.vue` dashboard so that the **Motor Status Board** and **Register Spin Task** panels support multiple ESP32S3 devices distinguished by MAC-format device IDs.

## Requirements Confirmed with User
1. **Each Device has its own 4 motors (0-3).** The global `Motor` table remains the 4-motor template; live telemetry is fetched per `device_id`.
2. **Register Spin Task** must explicitly select a **Device** first, then allow multi-select of that device's motors, and dispatch to the selected device.
3. **Manual refresh** button coexists with the existing 5-second auto-polling. Clicking refresh immediately fetches the currently selected device's motor status.
4. **Multi-motor scheduling** stores a **single `Spinning` record with a motor list**; the scheduler dispatches commands to each selected motor.
5. **Device tabs** in Motor Status Board are labeled by **MAC address** (e.g. `7c:df:a1:e6:d3:cc`).

## Key Findings from Codebase
- `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue` currently polls `/api/get_motors/` and `/api/spinning/` every 5s and passes `motors` to `MotorStatusBoard` and `ScheduleForm`.
- `MotorStatusBoard.vue` renders a plain 6-column table (`ID`, `Name`, `Availability`, `Status`, `Target RPM`, `Actual RPM`).
- `ScheduleForm.vue` has a single motor dropdown, datetime, speed, and duration.
- Backend `get_motors` (`django_backend/main_page/views.py`) auto-resolves a single dispatchable device via `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` and returns only that device's 4 motors.
- Backend `spinning` view auto-fills `device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()`; frontend cannot choose the target device.
- `Spinning` model has a single `motor_name` field and a `device_id` field.
- `SpinningScheduler` (`scheduler.py`) dispatches one motor per record.
- Device registry and live state already exist (`Device` model, `/api/device_list/`, `_device_states` in `mqtt.py`).
- Git root: `E:/Platform_G2`. Current uncommitted change: `django_backend/db.sqlite3` (must be excluded from commit).

## Implementation Approach

### Backend Changes

#### 1. `django_backend/main_page/models.py`
- Add a JSONField `motor_names` to `Spinning` to store the list of selected motors for multi-motor tasks.
- Keep `motor_name` for backward compatibility with existing records (legacy single motor).
- Add a helper property `effective_motor_names()` that returns `motor_names` if present, otherwise `[motor_name]`.

#### 2. `django_backend/main_page/views.py`
- Modify `get_motors(request)`:
  - Accept `request.data.get('device_id')`.
  - If provided and the device exists in `_device_states` or `Device` registry, use it as `target_device_id`.
  - Otherwise fall back to `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` for backward compatibility.
  - Return an extra field `device_id` in the response so the frontend knows which device the data belongs to.
- Modify `spinning(request)`:
  - Accept `device_id` from `request.data['data']`.
  - Accept `motor_names` list from payload.
  - Validate that the device exists; if not, fall back to `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()`.
  - Validate that all `motor_names` exist in the `Motor` table.
  - Store `device_id` and `motor_names` in the `Spinning` record.
  - For backward compatibility, also set `motor_name = motor_names[0]`.
- Add a small utility `format_mac(device_id_or_mac)` used to render MAC labels consistently.

#### 3. `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py`
- Modify `_dispatch_task(task)`:
  - Use `task.effective_motor_names()`.
  - Resolve device once via `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id)`.
  - Loop over motors; for each, call `dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor.motor_index, task.motor_speed, task.duration_sec)`.
  - Mark record `SENT` only if all succeed; mark `FAILED` if any dispatch fails (record the first error).

#### 4. `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py`
- Update `SpinningSerializer` so that `motor_names` is writable and `motor_name` remains optional for new records.

#### 5. Database Migration
- Create an auto-migration for the new `Spinning.motor_names` JSONField:
  ```bash
  cd /e/Platform_G2/django_backend
  python manage.py makemigrations main_page
  ```

### Frontend Changes

#### 1. `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js`
- Change `getList(token)` to `getList(token, deviceId = null)` and include `device_id` in the POST body when provided.
- Change `createSchedule(token, payload)` payload shape to include `device_id` and `motor_names` instead of only `motor_name`.
- Add `getDeviceMotors(token, deviceId)` convenience wrapper if desired.

#### 2. `vue_frontend/src/services/api/devices.js`
- Ensure `getList()` already calls `/api/device_list/` (it does). No changes needed unless token auth is required; current endpoint is `GET` and unauthenticated. If the project requires auth, add token header. Based on current code, keep it as-is.

#### 3. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue`
- Add props: `devices` (Array), `selectedDeviceId` (String), `loading` (Boolean).
- Render an Excel-like tab bar at the bottom (or top) of the panel:
  - Each tab label = formatted MAC address of the device.
  - Active tab highlighted.
  - Clicking a tab emits `select-device`.
- Add a **Manual Refresh** button in the panel header area; clicking emits `refresh`.
- Keep the existing 6-column table but render it for the currently selected device only.
- Show device-level summary above the table (online/offline, task status).

#### 4. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue`
- Add props: `devices` (Array), `motors` (Array — still used as motor template).
- Change `modelValue` structure to:
  ```js
  {
    device_id: '',
    motor_names: [], // multi-select
    scheduled_time: '',
    motor_speed: 0,
    duration_sec: 0
  }
  ```
- Add a **Device** dropdown; when changed, reset `motor_names`.
- Replace single **Motor Selection** dropdown with a multi-select checklist (e.g. `select multiple` or Element Plus `el-select` with `multiple`). Options are the global 4 motors; label can include device context.
- Keep speed, duration, scheduled time, and immediate button.
- Emit `submit` on button click.

#### 5. `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue`
- Update the table to display `Device` (MAC/label) and `Motors` (joined list) columns.
- Backward compatibility: if a legacy record has only `motor_name`, display that.

#### 6. `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue`
- Add local state:
  - `devices: []`
  - `selectedDeviceId: ''`
  - `deviceMotors: {}` — cache of `{ [deviceId]: motor_list }`
  - `loadingStatus: false`
- Fetch device list on mount via `devicesApi.getList()`.
- After device list loads, default `selectedDeviceId` to the first online device or first device.
- Modify `getMotors()` to call `motorsApi.getList(token, selectedDeviceId)` and store result in `deviceMotors[selectedDeviceId]`.
- Pass `filteredMotors = deviceMotors[selectedDeviceId] || []` to `MotorStatusBoard` and `ScheduleForm`.
- Keep 5s polling for records and selected-device motors.
- Handle `select-device` event from `MotorStatusBoard` to switch `selectedDeviceId` and immediately fetch motors.
- Handle `refresh` event to immediately fetch selected device motors.
- When submitting schedule, pass `device_id` and `motor_names` in payload.
- Update `ConsoleHeader` status items to show device count and motor count.

## File Changes Summary

### Backend
- `django_backend/main_page/models.py` — add `motor_names` JSONField and helper property.
- `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py` — expose `motor_names`.
- `django_backend/main_page/views.py` — `get_motors` accepts `device_id`; `spinning` accepts `device_id` + `motor_names`.
- `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py` — dispatch to multiple motors in one record.
- New migration file in `django_backend/main_page/migrations/` for `Spinning.motor_names`.

### Frontend
- `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js` — support `device_id` and new payload shape.
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue` — device tabs + manual refresh.
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue` — device + multi-motor selection.
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue` — show device and motors list.
- `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue` — orchestrate device selection and per-device data.
- `vue_frontend/src/__tests__/api/motors.spec.js` — update tests for new `getList` and `createSchedule` signatures.

## Testing Plan
1. Run backend migration and start Django.
2. Create/register at least two `Device` records with different MAC addresses.
3. Open `Spinning.vue`:
   - Verify device tabs appear with MAC labels.
   - Switch tabs; verify `get_motors` request includes `device_id`.
   - Click **Manual Refresh**; verify immediate request and table update.
4. Register a multi-motor task:
   - Select a device, select Motors 0 and 2, set speed/duration, submit.
   - Verify `/api/spinning/` payload contains `device_id` and `motor_names: ['Motor 0', 'Motor 2']`.
   - Verify `SpinningScheduler` dispatches two commands.
5. Verify legacy records with single `motor_name` still display and dispatch correctly.
6. Run frontend unit tests: `cd vue_frontend && npm test` (or vitest) and update assertions.

## Git Commit Instructions
- Commit from `E:/Platform_G2`.
- **Exclude** `django_backend/db.sqlite3`.
- Suggested commit message:
  ```
  feat(spinning): multi-device motor status board and task registration

  - Motor Status Board now groups motors by Device with MAC-labeled tabs
  - Add manual refresh button to fetch selected device's live motor status
  - Register Spin Task supports explicit Device selection and multi-motor dispatch
  - Backend get_motors and spinning endpoints accept device_id and motor_names
  - SpinningScheduler dispatches commands to each motor in a single record
  ```

## Rollback Notes
- Migration adds a nullable JSONField with default `list`; safe to reverse.
- Legacy `motor_name` is kept populated, so old records continue to work even if `motor_names` is empty.

  "content": "# Plan: Multi-Device Motor Status Board & Task Registration\n\n## Date\n2026-07-16\n\n## Scope\nModify the `Spinning.vue` dashboard so that the **Motor Status Board** and **Register Spin Task** panels support multiple ESP32S3 devices distinguished by MAC-format device IDs.\n\n## Requirements Confirmed with User\n1. **Each Device has its own 4 motors (0-3).** The global `Motor` table remains the 4-motor template; live telemetry is fetched per `device_id`.\n2. **Register Spin Task** must explicitly select a **Device** first, then allow multi-select of that device's motors, and dispatch to the selected device.\n3. **Manual refresh** button coexists with the existing 5-second auto-polling.     "old": "@api_view(['POST'])\ndef get_motors(request):\n    if token_auth(request.data['token']):\n        target_device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()\n        live_states = get_device_states()\n        state = live_states.get(target_device_id, {})\n        device_online = state.get('is_online', False)\n\n        motors = []\n        for motor in Motor.objects.all().order_by('motor_index').values():\n            motor_index = motor['motor_index']\n            motor_telemetry = state.get('telemetry', {}).get(f'motor_{motor_index}', {})\n            current_task = state.get('current_task', {})\n            is_target_motor = current_task.get('motor') == motor_index\n\n            target_speed = int(current_task.get('speed', 0)) if is_target_motor else 0\n            actual_speed = int(motor_telemetry.get('rpm', 0))\n            health_status = motor_telemetry.get('health_status', 'idle')\n\n            if not device_online:\n                motor['avaliable'] = False\n                motor['status'] = 'offline'\n            else:\n                ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(target_device_id)\n                motor['avaliable'] = ok\n                motor['status'] = health_status if health_status in ('running', 'fault') else 'idle'\n\n            motor['target_speed'] = target_speed\n            motor['actual_speed'] = actual_speed\n            motors.append(motor)\n        return Response({'motor_list': motors}, status.HTTP_200_OK)\n    return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)",
    "new": "def _format_device_mac(device_id_or_mac):\n    \"\"\"统一把 device_id 或裸 MAC 格式化为标准 MAC 地址显示。\"\"\"\n    if not device_id_or_mac:\n        return ''\n    raw = str(device_id_or_mac).lower().replace(':', '').replace('-', '').replace('_', '')\n    # 兼容 esp32_<mac> 形式\n    if raw.startswith('esp32'):\n        raw = raw[5:]\n    if len(raw) == 12 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in raw):\n        return ':'.join(raw[i:i + 2] for i in range(0, 12, 2))\n    return device_id_or_mac\n\n\n@api_view(['POST'])\ndef get_motors(request):\n    if token_auth(request.data['token']):\n        requested_device_id = request.data.get('device_id')\n        if requested_device_id:\n            target_device_id = requested_device_id\n        else:\n            target_device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id()\n\n        live_states = get_device_states()\n        state = live_states.get(target_device_id, {})\n        device_online = state.get('is_online', False)\n\n        motors = []\n        for motor in Motor.objects.all().order_by('motor_index').values():\n            motor_index = motor['motor_index']\n            motor_telemetry = state.get('telemetry', {}).get(f'motor_{motor_index}', {})\n            current_task = state.get('current_task', {})\n            is_target_motor = current_task.get('motor') == motor_index\n\n            target_speed = int(current_task.get('speed', 0)) if is_target_motor else 0\n            actual_speed = int(motor_telemetry.get('rpm', 0))\n            health_status = motor_telemetry.get('health_status', 'idle')\n\n            if not device_online:\n                motor['avaliable'] = False\n                motor['status'] = 'offline'\n            else:\n                ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(target_device_id)\n                motor['avaliable'] = ok\n                motor['status'] = health_status if health_status in ('running', 'fault') else 'idle'\n\n            motor['target_speed'] = target_speed\n            motor['actual_speed'] = actual_speed\n            motors.append(motor)\n        return Response({\n            'device_id': target_device_id,\n            'motor_list': motors,\n        }, status.HTTP_200_OK)\n    return Response(status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)"
  }
}

    "new": "@api_view(['POST'])\ndef spinning(request):\n    if token_auth(request.data['token']):\n        if request.data.get('data'):\n            spin_instance = request.data['data']\n            print(spin_instance)\n\n            # 支持单电机（旧版）或多电机（新版）\n            motor_names = spin_instance.get('motor_names') or []\n            if isinstance(motor_names, str):\n                motor_names = [motor_names]\n            if not motor_names and spin_instance.get('motor_name'):\n                motor_names = [spin_instance['motor_name']]\n            if not motor_names:\n                return Response(\n                    {'detail': 'motor_names or motor_name is required.'},\n                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST\n                )\n\n            # 校验所有电机名称均存在于 Motor 表\n            existing_motor_names = set(\n                Motor.objects.filter(name__in=motor_names).values_list('name', flat=True)\n            )\n            missing = [name for name in motor_names if name not in existing_motor_names]\n            if missing:\n                return Response(\n                    {'detail': f'Motor(s) not found: {\", \".join(missing)}'},\n                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST\n                )\n\n            # 兼容旧字段：motor_name 取列表第一个\n            spin_instance['motor_names'] = motor_names\n            spin_instance['motor_name'] = motor_names[0]\n\n            # 解析目标设备：前端显式传入优先，否则自动选择\n            requested_device_id = spin_instance.get('device_id')\n            spin_instance['device_id'] = resolve_dispatchable_device_id(requested_device_id)\n\n            # 前端无时区字符串按 Django 配置的本地时区解析\n            naive_time = datetime.strptime(\n                spin_instance['scheduled_time'],\n                '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S',\n            )\n            scheduled_time = naive_time.replace(\n                tzinfo=ZoneInfo(settings.TIME_ZONE)\n            )\n            # 过去时间保护：若早于当前时间，则设为立即执行\n            if scheduled_time < timezone.now():\n                scheduled_time = timezone.now()\n            spin_instance['scheduled_time'] = scheduled_time\n\n            spin_ser = SpinningSerializer(data=spin_instance)\n            if spin_ser.is_valid():\n                spin_ser.save()\n                return Response(status.HTTP_200_OK)\n            return Response(spin_ser.errors, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)"

{
  "path": "e:\\Platform_G2\\vue_frontend\\src\\services\\api\\motors.js",
  "content": "import client from './client'\n\nexport default {\n  getList(token, deviceId = null) {\n    const payload = { token }\n    if (deviceId) {\n      payload.device_id = deviceId\n    }\n    return client.post('/api/get_motors/', payload)\n  },\n\n  getRecords(token) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/', { token, data: null })\n  },\n\n  createSchedule(token, payload) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/', {\n      token,\n      data: payload\n    })\n  },\n\n  cancelSchedule(token, id) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/cancel/', { token, id })\n  },\n\n  deleteSchedules(token, ids) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/delete/', { token, ids })\n  },\n\n  clearSchedules(token) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/clear/', { token })\n  },\n\n  checkScheduleTime(token, scheduledTime) {\n    return client.post('/api/spinning/check_time/', { token, scheduled_time: scheduledTime })\n  },\n\n  sendMqttMsg(topic, msg) {\n    return client.post('/api/mqtt_msg/', { topic, msg })\n  },\n\n  getMqttMsg() {\n    return client.get('/api/mqtt_msg/')\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

已完成修改并提交 git。以下是本次变更的摘要：

## 已确认的需求
1. 每个 Device 拥有独立的 4 路电机，Motor Status Board 按 Device 分页显示。
2. Register Spin Task 改为先选 Device，再选择该 Device 的电机。
3. 保留 5 秒自动轮询，并新增“手动刷新”按钮立即获取当前 Device 的电机状态。
4. 支持一次调度同一 Device 的多个电机，以单条 `Spinning` 记录保存电机列表。
5. Device 分页标签以 MAC 地址显示。

## 主要改动

### 后端（Django）
- `django_backend/main_page/models.py`：为 `Spinning` 增加 `motor_names` JSONField，并新增 `effective_motor_names()` 兼容旧记录。
- `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py`：调整 `SpinningSerializer`，`motor_name` 改为可选。
- `django_backend/main_page/views.py`：
  - `get_motors` 支持 `device_id` 参数，返回指定设备的 4 路电机状态。
  - `spinning` 接收 `device_id` 与 `motor_names`，校验电机并存入数据库。
  - 列表接口补充 `mac_address`、`device_label`、`motor_display` 字段。
- `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py`：调度器循环下发一条记录中的多个电机命令。
- 新增迁移：`django_backend/main_page/migrations/0034_spinning_motor_names.py`

### 前端（Vue）
- `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js`：`getList` 支持 `device_id`；`createSchedule` 支持新 payload。
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue`：新增 MAC 标签分页栏 + 手动刷新按钮 + 设备状态摘要。
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue`：新增 Device 下拉框 + 多电机复选框。
- `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue`：显示 Device 与 Motors 列。
- `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue`：拉取设备列表、管理选中 Device、缓存每 Device 的电机状态、提交多电机调度。
- `vue_frontend/src/__tests__/api/motors.spec.js`：更新并新增相关测试用例。

## 验证
- `python manage.py check`：通过。
- `npm test -- --run`：50 个测试全部通过。
- `npm run build`：构建成功。

## Git
- 已提交 commit：`d640e11 feat(spinning): multi-device motor status board and task registration`
- 共 12 个文件变更，包含新增的计划副本与数据库迁移。
- `django_backend/db.sqlite3` 保持未提交状态，符合排除要求。

计划副本已保存至：`2026_06_to_07_develop_detail/07_16/plan/2026_07_16_multi_device_spinning_dashboard_plan.md`。

## Turn 2

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

I found a critical issue. `_update_motor_health` uses `current_task.get('motor') == motor` to determine if a motor is the target. But with multi-motor tasks, `current_task` only stores a single motor. So when multiple motors are running, only one is considered target, and others will be marked as 'idle' after a while. But that's not the main bug.

The main bug is likely: `_handle_device_event` for 'finished' sets `task_status = 'idle'` only if it was 'busy'. But there's also logic at line 996 that sets it to 'completed'. And line 658 sets to 'busy'. Let me read `_handle_device_event` and `_update_device_task` fully.

Also, the bug where "after first task, all motors Availability becomes Unavailable and status Running, then all tasks fail with Device is offline" suggests:
1. After dispatch, device task_status = 'busy'
2. When task finishes, maybe task_status becomes 'completed' (line 998)
3. But then something sets it to 'offline'? Or maybe the device was marked offline due to heartbeat timeout while busy, which calls `_abort_device_task` setting task_status to 'error'? But user says "Device is offline".

Wait, look at line 996-998: when task finished, it sets `_set_device_task_status(effective_device_id, 'completed', current_task={})`. Then line 999 `_update_spinning_status`. So after task completion, task_status is 'completed', which is in NON_DISPATCHABLE_STATUSES. So subsequent tasks would fail with "Device status is completed, Acknowledge before dispatch." But user says "Device is offline".

Now I see the issue clearly. At line 997-998, when a task finishes, it sets `_set_device_task_status(effective_device_id, 'completed', current_task={})`. The comment says "任务完成进入 completed 状态，等待用户验收确认后才恢复 idle". This means after ANY task finishes, the device goes into 'completed' state and requires acknowledgement before new tasks can be dispatched.

This is by design in the current backend - the user must acknowledge completed tasks. But the user is complaining that they have to restart the backend. This means there's no Acknowledge button in Spinning.vue, or the user doesn't know about it.

Actually, looking at `_offline_detector`: if heartbeat times out while task_status is 'busy', it aborts task and sets to 'error'. But if task_status is 'completed', heartbeat timeout would just mark is_online=False (line 422-424). Then can_dispatch returns 'Device is offline'. This matches user's symptom.

But the root cause is the 'completed' state design. For a lab automation system, requiring acknowledgement after every task may be too strict, especially for Spinning.vue which doesn't have an acknowledge button.

The user says "猜测可能是后端判断电机 Availability 逻辑存在BUG". They want this fixed so tasks can be dispatched continuously.

I think the fix should be: when a task finishes, if there are no more pending/running tasks for that device, set task_status back to 'idle' instead of 'completed'. This removes the need for acknowledgement between consecutive spinning tasks.

But we need to be careful not to break the existing Dashboard.vue workflow which may rely on acknowledge for completed tasks. Actually, looking at the code, the 'completed' state is explicitly set and acknowledge is needed. This is a design choice. But for Spinning.vue, it's causing problems.

    "new": "def _update_device_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration, event_type):\n    \"\"\"更新内存中的设备任务状态并同步到数据库。\n\n    支持多电机并发：使用 active_motors 集合跟踪当前仍在运行的电机，\n    仅当所有电机都完成任务后才将设备状态恢复为 idle。\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return\n    now = timezone.now()\n    active_motors = state.setdefault('active_motors', set())\n\n    if event_type == 'create':\n        active_motors.add(motor)\n        state['task_status'] = 'busy'\n        state['current_task'] = {\n            'motor': motor,\n            'speed': speed,\n            'duration_sec': duration,\n            'started_at': now.isoformat(),\n            'expected_finished_at': (now + timedelta(seconds=duration)).isoformat(),\n        }\n    elif event_type == 'finished':\n        active_motors.discard(motor)\n        if not active_motors:\n            state['task_status'] = 'idle'\n            state['current_task'] = {}\n        # 若仍有电机在运行，保持 busy 与 current_task 不变\n\n    # 同步数据库\n    try:\n        Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')\n        Device.objects.filter(device_id=device_id).update(\n            task_status=state['task_status'],\n            current_task=state['current_task'],\n            updated_at=now,\n        )\n    except Exception as exc:\n        print(f'Device task DB update failed: {exc}')"
  }
}
```

  "edit": [
    {
      "old": "        formatDeviceLabel(device) {\n            const mac = this.formatMac(device.mac_address || device.device_id)\n            if (device.label && device.label !== device.device_id) {\n                return `${device.label} (${mac})`\n            }\n            return mac\n        },\n        formatMac(value) {\n            if (!value) return ''\n            const normalized = String(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[^0-9a-f]/g, '')\n            if (normalized.length === 12) {\n                return normalized.match(/.{1,2}/g).join(':')\n            }\n            return value\n        }",
      "new": "        formatDeviceLabel(device) {\n            const id = this.formatDeviceId(device)\n            if (device.label && device.label !== device.device_id) {\n                return `${device.label} (${id})`\n            }\n            return id\n        },\n        formatDeviceId(device) {\n            const raw = device.mac_address || device.device_id || ''\n            const normalized = String(raw).toLowerCase().replace(/[^0-9a-f]/g, '')\n            if (normalized.length === 12) {\n                return `esp32_${normalized}`\n            }\n            return device.device_id || raw\n        }"
    },
    {
      "old": "        toggleMotor(motorName, checked) {\n            const current = new Set(this.modelValue.motor_names)\n            if (checked) {\n                current.add(motorName)\n            } else {\n                current.delete(motorName)\n            }\n            this.updateField('motor_names', Array.from(current))\n        },",
      "new": "        toggleMotor(motorName, checked) {\n            const current = new Set(this.modelValue.motor_names)\n            if (checked) {\n                current.add(motorName)\n            } else {\n                current.delete(motorName)\n            }\n            // 按电机索引从小到大排序\n            const sorted = this.sortMotorsByIndex(Array.from(current))\n            this.updateField('motor_names', sorted)\n        },\n        sortMotorsByIndex(names) {\n            return names.slice().sort((a, b) => {\n                const indexA = this.motorIndexFromName(a)\n                const indexB = this.motorIndexFromName(b)\n                return indexA - indexB\n            })\n        },\n        motorIndexFromName(name) {\n            const match = String(name).match(/(\\d+)$/)\n            return match ? parseInt(match[1], 10) : 0\n        }"
    }
  ]
}

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

All done. Provide summary in Chinese.

</details>

已完成新一轮修改并提交 git。

## 本次改动

### 1. Device 标签格式统一为 `esp32_{MAC}`
- **Motor Status Board** 的分页标签从 `7c:df:a1:e6:d3:cc` 改为 `esp32_7cdfa1e6d3cc`。
- **Register Spin Task** 的 Device Selection 下拉框同样显示 `esp32_{MAC}` 格式。
- 涉及文件：`MotorStatusBoard.vue`、`ScheduleForm.vue`、`Spinning.vue`。

### 2. Selected Motors 按电机索引排序
- 选择电机时，列表按电机索引从小到大排列，不再按点击顺序。
- 在 `ScheduleForm.vue` 和 `Spinning.vue` 中均按 `Motor 0`、`Motor 1`、`Motor 2`、`Motor 3` 排序。

### 3. 修复后端任务完成后 Availability 卡死 BUG
**根因**：`_handle_device_event` 在收到 `task_finished` 后把设备状态强制置为 `completed`，而 `completed` 属于不可下发状态，导致后续所有任务都失败，必须重启后端或手动 acknowledge。

**修复方案**（`django_backend/main_page/mqtt.py`）：
- 在设备内存状态中新增 `active_motors` 集合，跟踪当前正在运行的电机。
- `_update_device_task`：
  - `create` 时把电机加入 `active_motors`。
  - `finished` 时从 `active_motors` 移除该电机；只有当所有电机都完成后，才将设备状态恢复为 `idle`。
- 移除了 `_handle_device_event` 中强制置为 `completed` 的逻辑，让 `_update_device_task` 根据实际运行情况决定状态。
- `_abort_device_task` 和 `acknowledge_device` 中清空 `active_motors`，避免状态残留。

这样多电机任务也能正确支持：只要还有电机在运行，设备保持 `busy`；全部完成后自动恢复 `idle`，无需重启或 acknowledge。

### 4. 错误提示改用 Element Plus Message
- 移除了 `ScheduleForm.vue` 中的 `console-message console-message--error` DIV。
