<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

OK, so heartbeat is updated on any message from device including data and heartbeat. Good.

Now back to the user's issue: after first task, device shows offline. Why?

Possibility 1: The WebSocket error with set serialization causes the consumer to crash and somehow affects the state? No, WebSocket is separate.

Possibility 2: When task is dispatched via scheduler, `_set_device_task_status` sets busy. Then ESP32 runs task. But if the ESP32 firmware doesn't send `task_finished`, the active_motors never clears and task_status stays busy. But user says status shows Running and Actual RPM not changing. If Actual RPM not changing, maybe the motor actually stopped but telemetry is stale? Or the ESP32 is still sending the old RPM value?

Wait, user says "status 一直显示 Running, Actual RPM 无变化". This suggests the health_status is 'running' but rpm is not updating. This happens when `current_task.get('motor') == motor` and target_speed > 0. If current_task still points to that motor, health_status stays running even if rpm=0 (until zero_samples reaches threshold).

But the "Device is offline" error means is_online=False. If device is offline, can_dispatch returns offline. But the frontend might still show status from cached telemetry.

Hmm. Actually, I think the issue might be simpler. Let me re-read the scheduler dispatch loop:

```python
device_id = resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id)
result = dispatch_motor_task(
    device_id,
    motor.motor_index,
    task.motor_speed,
    task.duration_sec,
)
```

For multi-motor, it loops over motors and calls `dispatch_motor_task` for each. But `dispatch_motor_task` calls `can_dispatch_to_device` at the beginning. After the first motor is dispatched, the device is 'busy'. Then for the second motor, `can_dispatch_to_device` returns False because task_status is 'busy'! So only the first motor gets dispatched, and the scheduler marks the record as FAILED.

Wait, but the user said "第一次下发任务之后" - they might be using a single motor task. Let me think about single motor.

For single motor task:
1. Scheduler calls dispatch_motor_task(device_id, 0, speed, duration)
2. can_dispatch passes, device goes busy
3. MQTT command sent
4. ESP32 receives, sends task_create_0_speed_duration
5. _update_device_task sets active_motors={0}, current_task updated
6. ESP32 runs motor, sends pcnt_rpm telemetry
7. _update_device_heartbeat called, is_online=True
8. Task finishes, ESP32 sends task_finished_0_speed_duration
9. _update_device_task removes 0 from active_motors, active_motors empty, task_status=idle
10. Device should be dispatchable again

So why would it show offline? Unless the ESP32 is not sending heartbeats/data after task finishes, and the 90s timeout marks it offline.

Actually wait, the user says "Actual RPM 无变化" - this could mean the motor stopped sending telemetry, so no heartbeat update, so after 90s it goes offline. But why would the motor stop sending telemetry after one task? Maybe the ESP32 firmware crashed or stopped? Or the MQTT connection dropped?

Or maybe the issue is that after dispatch, the current_task is set to the last motor, and `_update_motor_health` checks `current_task.get('motor') == motor`. For multi-motor, only the last motor's current_task is stored, so other motors might not show running. But that's separate.

Actually, I think there might be a bug in the heartbeat logic. Let me look at `_extract_device_id_from_topic` and the topic subscription. Maybe the backend isn't receiving heartbeat messages.

Wait, the user says after restart, "需要经过一段时间才显示 Availability:Available；Status：Idle". This is expected because it needs to receive the first heartbeat/data message. Before any message, is_online=False and task_status='idle', so can_dispatch returns False with "Device is offline".

But the issue is after the FIRST task, it goes offline. This suggests the device was online, task ran, then device stopped sending messages.

OR - and this is important - maybe the issue is that `dispatch_motor_task` sets task_status to 'busy', and then `can_dispatch_to_device` returns 'Device is busy' for subsequent tasks. But the user says the log shows "Device is offline". Unless the scheduler logs the reason differently.

Wait, let me look at the scheduler error logging. In `_dispatch_task`, if `result.get('success')` is False, it raises RuntimeError(result.get('error', 'Unknown dispatch error')). So the error message is whatever dispatch_motor_task returned. If can_dispatch returns (False, 'Device is offline'), that's what gets logged.

So the device is genuinely considered offline. Why?

Let me think about the heartbeat timeout. HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 90. The offline detector runs every 15s. If the ESP32 sends heartbeats every... what interval? The ESP32 heartbeat task is 30s interval (from AGENTS.md: MQTT heartbeat sender 30s interval). So it should not timeout.

But wait, the user says "Actual RPM 无变化" - maybe the motor is running but RPM telemetry stopped. If telemetry stopped, heartbeat might also stop. If the ESP32 is still connected but not sending data, it would be marked offline after 90s.

Actually, I wonder if the issue is related to the ESP32 firmware's command handling. When a new command arrives while a previous command is running, the ESP32 deletes the old task and creates a new one. But maybe there's a race condition.

Hmm, but this is a backend issue according to the user. Let me focus on what we can fix in the backend.

One possible issue: when the scheduler dispatches a multi-motor task, after dispatching the first motor, the second motor's dispatch fails because device is busy. This could leave the device in a weird state. But user is testing single motor perhaps.

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Yes, `send_snapshot` copies state with `**state`, which includes `active_motors: set()`. Then `json.dumps` fails. This is the first bug.

Fix: Convert `active_motors` to a list in `send_snapshot`, or better, in `get_device_states` return a serializable copy. I think the cleanest fix is in `get_device_states` to convert sets to lists.

Now for the second bug, let me think more carefully.

Actually, I just realized something. The user said "第一次在第一次下发任务之后，所有电机 Availability 直接被显示为 Unavailable，且之后无法正常下发任务，后端日志显示 Device is offline".

Maybe the issue is: the scheduler dispatches a multi-motor task. After the first motor is dispatched, `dispatch_motor_task` sets task_status='busy'. Then the second motor dispatch fails because can_dispatch says 'Device is busy'. The scheduler catches this and marks the Spinning record as FAILED.

But the user says "Device is offline", not "Device is busy". Hmm.

Wait, could the issue be in the frontend? The frontend fetches motors via `get_motors`. If the device is busy, `can_dispatch_to_device` returns False, so `motor['avaliable'] = ok` is False. So all motors show Unavailable. The reason isn't shown in the table. Then when trying to submit a new task, `spinning` view calls `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(requested_device_id)`. If the requested device is busy/offline, it falls back to `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` which tries to find any online+idle device. If none found, it returns the default device_id (e.g. esp32_1) but that device might not exist or be offline, so dispatch fails with "Device is offline".

Actually wait, `resolve_dispatchable_device_id` with preferred_device_id:
```python
def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None):
    if preferred_device_id:
        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)
        if ok:
            return preferred_device_id
    # try all devices...
    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')
```

If preferred device is busy, it tries other devices. If none are dispatchable, it returns MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID. Then `dispatch_motor_task` is called with that device_id. If that device doesn't exist or is offline, can_dispatch returns 'Device is offline'.

So the user's actual issue might be:
1. They select device A and submit task.
2. Task runs, device A becomes busy.
3. They submit another task to device A.
4. `spinning` view: requested_device_id = A, but A is busy, so resolve_dispatchable_device_id falls back.
5. If no other device is available, returns default esp32_1.
6. dispatch_motor_task(esp32_1, ...) fails with 'Device is offline' because esp32_1 is offline/non-existent.

This would explain the "Device is offline" error. But the user says "实际存在电机模块可用" - maybe they mean the same device is physically available but backend thinks it's busy.

Actually, the root issue is that after a task finishes, the device should return to idle, but maybe it's not happening. Let me verify the active_motors logic works correctly for single motor.

Wait, there's another issue. `_update_device_task` on 'finished' removes motor from active_motors. But `dispatch_motor_task` initially sets `current_task` via `_set_device_task_status`. Then `_update_device_task` on 'create' overwrites it. On 'finished', if active_motors becomes empty, it sets task_status='idle' and current_task={}. This should work.

But what if the ESP32 firmware doesn't send `task_finished`? Then active_motors stays non-empty, task_status stays busy. But user says "status 一直显示 Running" - motor health_status running, which means current_task still points to that motor. This would happen if task_finished is not received.

But why would Actual RPM not change? If the motor is actually running, RPM should change. If it's stopped, RPM should be 0. "Actual RPM 无变化" might mean the display is frozen at the last value because no new telemetry is coming.

So the device might actually be offline or not communicating. But user says it exists and is available.

Hmm. Let me look at the actual ESP32 firmware. Does it send `task_finished`? Looking at AGENTS.md, the project uses `cmd_<motor>_<speed>_<duration>`. The firmware runs the command for duration seconds then stops. It may or may not send task_finished. Let me search for task_finished in the ESP32 code.

Actually, the task_finished is probably sent by firmware. Let me check.

```json
    "new": "def _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id, grace_seconds=30):\n    \"\"\"若设备长期处于 busy 且已远超预期完成时间，自动释放为 idle，避免状态死锁。\"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return\n    if state.get('task_status') != 'busy':\n        return\n    current_task = state.get('current_task') or {}\n    expected_finished_at = current_task.get('expected_finished_at')\n    if not expected_finished_at:\n        return\n    try:\n        expected = datetime.fromisoformat(expected_finished_at)\n    except Exception:\n        return\n    if timezone.now() > expected + timedelta(seconds=grace_seconds):\n        print(f'Auto-release stale busy state for {device_id} (expected finished at {expected_finished_at})')\n        _set_device_task_status(device_id, 'idle', current_task={})\n        state['active_motors'] = set()\n\n\ndef can_dispatch_to_device(device_id):\n    \"\"\"\n    判断是否可以向指定 device_id 下发新任务。\n    返回 (ok, reason)。\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return False, 'Unknown device'\n    # 防御性清理：若 busy 状态已过期，先自动释放\n    _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id)\n    if not state.get('is_online', False):\n        return False, 'Device is offline'\n    task_status = state.get('task_status', 'idle')\n    if task_status in NON_DISPATCHABLE_STATUSES:\n        status_label = {\n            'busy': 'Device is busy',\n            'estopped': 'Device is in emergency stop state. Resume before dispatch.',\n            'error': 'Device has an error. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'completed': 'Device has a completed task. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'offline': 'Device is offline',\n        }\n        return False, status_label.get(task_status, f'Device status is {task_status}')\n    return True, ''"
  }
}
```

```json
    "old": "def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    解析当前可用于下发任务的设备 ID。\n    优先使用 preferred_device_id；若其不可下发，则遍历所有已注册设备，\n    通过实时内存状态 (can_dispatch_to_device) 找到第一个在线且空闲的设备；\n    若都没有，则回退到 settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID。\n    \"\"\"\n    if preferred_device_id:\n        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)\n        if ok:\n            return preferred_device_id\n\n    try:\n        Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')\n        # 遍历所有已注册设备，使用实时内存状态判断，而不是仅依赖 DB 的 is_online 字段\n        for device in Device.objects.all().order_by('device_id'):\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(device.device_id)\n            if ok:\n                return device.device_id\n    except Exception as exc:\n        print(f'Resolve dispatchable device failed: {exc}')\n\n    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')",
    "new": "def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    解析当前可用于下发任务的设备 ID。\n    优先使用 preferred_device_id；若其不可下发，则遍历所有已注册设备，\n    通过实时内存状态 (can_dispatch_to_device) 找到第一个在线且空闲的设备；\n    若都没有，则回退到 settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID。\n    \"\"\"\n    if preferred_device_id:\n        _auto_release_stale_busy_state(preferred_device_id)\n        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)\n        if ok:\n            return preferred_device_id\n\n    try:\n        Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')\n        # 遍历所有已注册设备，使用实时内存状态判断，而不是仅依赖 DB 的 is_online 字段\n        for device in Device.objects.all().order_by('device_id'):\n            _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device.device_id)\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(device.device_id)\n            if ok:\n                return device.device_id\n    except Exception as exc:\n        print(f'Resolve dispatchable device failed: {exc}')\n\n    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')"
```

```json
{

  "edit": {
    "old": "def dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration):\n    \"\"\"向指定设备下发电机任务；下发前检查设备在线与空闲状态。\"\"\"\n    ok, reason = can_dispatch_to_device(device_id)\n    if not ok:\n        return {'success': False, 'error': reason}\n    if client is None or not client.is_connected():\n        return {'success': False, 'error': 'MQTT client unavailable'}\n\n    topic = _device_control_topic(device_id)\n    cmd = f'cmd_{motor}_{speed}_{duration}'\n    now = timezone.now()\n    expected_finished_at = now + timedelta(seconds=duration)\n\n    # 乐观置为 busy，防止并发下发；设备后续上报 task_create 时会再次刷新 current_task\n    _set_device_task_status(\n        device_id,\n        'busy',\n        current_task={\n            'motor': motor,\n            'speed': speed,\n            'duration_sec': duration,\n            'started_at': now.isoformat(),\n            'expected_finished_at': expected_finished_at.isoformat(),\n        },\n    )\n\n    try:\n        info = client.publish(topic, cmd)\n        return {'success': True, 'topic': topic, 'command': cmd, 'rc': info.rc}\n    except Exception as exc:\n        # 下发失败回滚为 idle，避免一直占用\n        _set_device_task_status(device_id, 'idle', current_task={})\n        return {'success': False, 'error': str(exc)}",
    "new": "def dispatch_motor_task(device_id, motor, speed, duration, check_dispatch=True):\n    \"\"\"向指定设备下发电机任务。\n\n    参数 check_dispatch：默认 True，下发前检查设备在线与空闲状态；\n    用于多电机调度时，首个电机已确认可下发，后续电机直接发布命令。\n    \"\"\"\n    if check_dispatch:\n        ok, reason = can_dispatch_to_device(device_id)\n        if not ok:\n            return {'success': False, 'error': reason}\n    if client is None or not client.is_connected():\n        return {'success': False, 'error': 'MQTT client unavailable'}\n\n    topic = _device_control_topic(device_id)\n    cmd = f'cmd_{motor}_{speed}_{duration}'\n    now = timezone.now()\n    expected_finished_at = now + timedelta(seconds=duration)\n\n    # 乐观置为 busy，防止并发下发；设备后续上报 task_create 时会再次刷新 current_task\n    _set_device_task_status(\n        device_id,\n        'busy',\n        current_task={\n            'motor': motor,\n            'speed': speed,\n            'duration_sec': duration,\n            'started_at': now.isoformat(),\n            'expected_finished_at': expected_finished_at.isoformat(),\n        },\n    )\n\n    try:\n        info = client.publish(topic, cmd)\n        return {'success': True, 'topic': topic, 'command': cmd, 'rc': info.rc}\n    except Exception as exc:\n        # 下发失败回滚为 idle，避免一直占用\n        _set_device_task_status(device_id, 'idle', current_task={})\n        return {'success': False, 'error': str(exc)}"
  }
}
```

已完成 BUG 修复并提交 git。

## 修复内容

### 1. WebSocket 序列化错误
**原因**：`get_device_states()` 返回的设备内存状态中包含 `active_motors: set()`，`json.dumps` 无法序列化 `set`。

**修复**：在 `django_backend/main_page/mqtt.py` 的 `get_device_states()` 中将 `active_motors` 转换为排序后的 `list`，确保 WebSocket 快照和 API 序列化正常。

### 2. 第一次下发任务后 Availability 变 Unavailable / 后续任务提示 Device is offline
**原因分析**：
- 设备被调度器置为 `busy` 后，若因网络、固件或其他原因未收到 `task_finished`，`busy` 状态会永久占用，导致后续所有 `can_dispatch_to_device()` 返回失败。
- 多电机任务中，调度器逐个电机调用 `dispatch_motor_task()`，第一个电机成功后设备变 `busy`，第二个电机再检查 `can_dispatch` 时被拦截，导致多电机任务失败。

**修复**：
- **busy 状态超时自动释放**：新增 `_auto_release_stale_busy_state()`，在 `can_dispatch_to_device()` 和 `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` 中调用。若设备长期处于 `busy` 且已超过 `expected_finished_at + 30s` 宽限期，自动恢复为 `idle`。
- **多电机连续下发修复**：`dispatch_motor_task()` 新增 `check_dispatch` 参数，默认 `True`。调度器对多电机任务的首个电机检查设备状态，后续电机直接发布 MQTT 命令，避免被自己的 `busy` 状态拦截。

### 3. 涉及文件
- `django_backend/main_page/mqtt.py`
- `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py`

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Backend subscribes to `esp32/+/+` which matches `esp32/<mac>/task`, so task_create and task_finished messages are received. Good.

Now the main task: change availability logic to per-motor.

Current `get_motors` logic:
```python
ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(target_device_id)
motor['avaliable'] = ok
```

This sets all motors' availability based on device-level can_dispatch. I need to change it to per-motor availability: a motor is available if the device is online AND that motor is not in active_motors.

Also, `can_dispatch_to_device` is used by `dispatch_motor_task` and scheduler. For dispatch, we should check if the specific motor is available. But `dispatch_motor_task` takes a motor index. So we can add a new function `can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index)` or modify `can_dispatch_to_device` to optionally accept motor index.

Plan:
1. Add `can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index)` function that checks:
   - Device exists
   - Device is online
   - Device task_status is not in non-dispatchable states (estopped, error, completed, offline)
   - If task_status is busy, the requested motor must not be in active_motors
2. Modify `get_motors` to use per-motor availability.
3. Modify `dispatch_motor_task` to use `can_dispatch_motor` with the specific motor.
4. Modify scheduler's first motor dispatch to use per-motor check.
5. Clear RPM when motor becomes idle: in `_update_device_task` on finished, set telemetry rpm=0 for that motor and health_status=idle. Also when device goes offline or heartbeat timeout.
6. Also handle the case where task_finished is received but RPM stays stale.

Actually, for clearing RPM: when task_finished is received, the firmware stops the motor and sets pwm to 8191 (off). But the last RPM telemetry might be non-zero. The backend should set rpm=0 in telemetry when task_finished is received for that motor. This ensures Actual RPM shows 0 after task ends.

Also, when device goes offline, we might want to set all motors' rpm=0 and status=offline.

Let me also think about multi-motor: if motor 0 is running, motor 1 should still be available. With per-motor availability, this works.

Let me implement these changes.

```json
    "old": "def can_dispatch_to_device(device_id):\n    \"\"\"\n    判断是否可以向指定 device_id 下发新任务。\n    返回 (ok, reason)。\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return False, 'Unknown device'\n    # 防御性清理：若 busy 状态已过期，先自动释放\n    _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id)\n    if not state.get('is_online', False):\n        return False, 'Device is offline'\n    task_status = state.get('task_status', 'idle')\n    if task_status in NON_DISPATCHABLE_STATUSES:\n        status_label = {\n            'busy': 'Device is busy',\n            'estopped': 'Device is in emergency stop state. Resume before dispatch.',\n            'error': 'Device has an error. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'completed': 'Device has a completed task. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'offline': 'Device is offline',\n        }\n        return False, status_label.get(task_status, f'Device status is {task_status}')\n    return True, ''",
    "new": "def can_dispatch_to_device(device_id):\n    \"\"\"\n    判断是否可以向指定 device_id 下发新任务（设备级）。\n    返回 (ok, reason)。\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return False, 'Unknown device'\n    # 防御性清理：若 busy 状态已过期，先自动释放\n    _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id)\n    if not state.get('is_online', False):\n        return False, 'Device is offline'\n    task_status = state.get('task_status', 'idle')\n    if task_status in NON_DISPATCHABLE_STATUSES:\n        status_label = {\n            'busy': 'Device is busy',\n            'estopped': 'Device is in emergency stop state. Resume before dispatch.',\n            'error': 'Device has an error. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'completed': 'Device has a completed task. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'offline': 'Device is offline',\n        }\n        return False, status_label.get(task_status, f'Device status is {task_status}')\n    return True, ''\n\n\ndef can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index):\n    \"\"\"\n    判断是否可以向指定设备的指定电机下发新任务（电机级）。\n    当设备处于 busy 状态时，只要目标电机本身不在 active_motors 中，仍然可下发。\n    返回 (ok, reason)。\n    \"\"\"\n    state = _ensure_device_state(device_id)\n    if state is None:\n        return False, 'Unknown device'\n    _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id)\n    if not state.get('is_online', False):\n        return False, 'Device is offline'\n    task_status = state.get('task_status', 'idle')\n    if task_status in ('estopped', 'error', 'completed', 'offline'):\n        status_label = {\n            'estopped': 'Device is in emergency stop state. Resume before dispatch.',\n            'error': 'Device has an error. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'completed': 'Device has a completed task. Acknowledge before dispatch.',\n            'offline': 'Device is offline',\n        }\n        return False, status_label.get(task_status, f'Device status is {task_status}')\n    if task_status == 'busy':\n        active_motors = state.get('active_motors', set())\n        if motor_index in active_motors:\n            return False, f'Motor {motor_index} is busy'\n    return True, ''"
```

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Now update scheduler to use can_dispatch_motor for the first motor instead of relying on resolve_dispatchable_device_id's implicit check. Actually, resolve_dispatchable_device_id now uses can_dispatch_to_device which is device-level. But for multi-motor, we want per-motor. However, resolve_dispatchable_device_id only resolves the device, not the motor. Then dispatch_motor_task checks can_dispatch_motor.

But if device is busy with motor 0, and we want to dispatch motor 1, resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id) will check can_dispatch_to_device(device_id). Since device is busy, it will NOT return preferred_device_id; it will fall back to other devices. This is wrong! We want to keep the same device because motor 1 is available.

So I need to modify `resolve_dispatchable_device_id` to accept a motor_index parameter, or change the scheduler logic to not use resolve_dispatchable_device_id for preferred device when per-motor availability matters.

Better approach: modify `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None, motor_index=None)`. When motor_index is provided, use `can_dispatch_motor` instead of `can_dispatch_to_device` for preferred device. For fallback to other devices, still use can_dispatch_to_device (or can_dispatch_motor if we know the motor).

But in scheduler, we know the motor index for each motor. So we can call `resolve_dispatchable_device_id(task.device_id, motors[0].motor_index)`.

Let me modify resolve_dispatchable_device_id.

```json
{
  "path": "e:\\Platform_G2\\django_backend\\main_page\\mqtt.py",
  "edit": {
    "old": "def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    解析当前可用于下发任务的设备 ID。\n    优先使用 preferred_device_id；若其不可下发，则遍历所有已注册设备，\n    通过实时内存状态 (can_dispatch_to_device) 找到第一个在线且空闲的设备；\n    若都没有，则回退到 settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID。\n    \"\"\"\n    if preferred_device_id:\n        _auto_release_stale_busy_state(preferred_device_id)\n        ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)\n        if ok:\n            return preferred_device_id\n\n    try:\n        Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')\n        # 遍历所有已注册设备，使用实时内存状态判断，而不是仅依赖 DB 的 is_online 字段\n        for device in Device.objects.all().order_by('device_id'):\n            _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device.device_id)\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(device.device_id)\n            if ok:\n                return device.device_id\n    except Exception as exc:\n        print(f'Resolve dispatchable device failed: {exc}')\n\n    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')",
    "new": "def resolve_dispatchable_device_id(preferred_device_id=None, motor_index=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    解析当前可用于下发任务的设备 ID。\n    优先使用 preferred_device_id；若 motor_index 提供，则按电机级可用性判断；\n    否则按设备级可用性判断。若首选不可用，则遍历所有已注册设备寻找可用设备；\n    若都没有，则回退到 settings.MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID。\n    \"\"\"\n    dispatch_check = can_dispatch_motor if motor_index is not None else can_dispatch_to_device\n\n    if preferred_device_id:\n        _auto_release_stale_busy_state(preferred_device_id)\n        if motor_index is not None:\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_motor(preferred_device_id, motor_index)\n        else:\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(preferred_device_id)\n        if ok:\n            return preferred_device_id\n\n    try:\n        Device = apps.get_model('main_page', 'Device')\n        # 遍历所有已注册设备，使用实时内存状态判断，而不是仅依赖 DB 的 is_online 字段\n        for device in Device.objects.all().order_by('device_id'):\n            _auto_release_stale_busy_state(device.device_id)\n            ok, _ = can_dispatch_to_device(device.device_id)\n            if ok:\n                return device.device_id\n    except Exception as exc:\n        print(f'Resolve dispatchable device failed: {exc}')\n\n    return getattr(settings, 'MQTT_DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID', 'esp32_1')"
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Now, I need to make sure that the `_update_device_task` on 'finished' clears RPM. I already added that. But it only clears if motor_key exists in telemetry. If telemetry doesn't exist for that motor, it does nothing. That's fine.

But wait, there's a subtle issue. `_update_device_task` on 'finished' clears RPM and sets health_status='idle'. But `_update_motor_health` is called on every pcnt_rpm message. After task_finished, the motor stops, so RPM becomes 0. The next pcnt_rpm with rpm=0 would set health_status to 'idle' anyway. But clearing immediately on task_finished is better.

Now, there's another issue: `_update_motor_health` checks `current_task.get('motor') == motor`. For multi-motor, current_task only stores the last motor. So for motor 0 running, if current_task points to motor 0, health_status running. If motor 1 also running, current_task gets overwritten to motor 1. Then motor 0's health_status might be set to idle incorrectly when rpm=0 (but it's actually running). This is a problem for multi-motor.

To fix this, `_update_motor_health` should check if the motor is in `active_motors` instead of comparing to current_task.motor. Let me update it.

</details>

已完成修改并提交 git。

## 本次改动

### 1. 电机级 Availability（核心修复）
之前逻辑是**设备级**判断：只要设备上有一个电机 busy，所有电机都显示 `Unavailable`。现在改为**电机级**判断：

- 新增 `can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index)`：
  - 设备在线；
  - 设备未处于 `estopped` / `error` / `completed` / `offline`；
  - 若设备为 `busy`，只要**目标电机**不在 `active_motors` 中，仍然可下发。
- `get_motors()` 现在对每个电机单独调用 `can_dispatch_motor()`，因此一个电机运行时不影响其他空闲电机的 `Available` 状态。
- `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` 新增 `motor_index` 参数，调度器在解析目标设备时使用电机级可用性，避免 busy 电机把整台设备挤掉。
- `dispatch_motor_task()` 使用 `can_dispatch_motor()` 做单电机校验。

### 2. 任务结束后 `Actual RPM` 清零
- 后端收到 `task_finished_...` 时，立即将该电机的 `telemetry.rpm` 置 0、`health_status` 置 `idle`，前端 `Actual RPM` 会立即归零而不是卡住旧值。
- 乐观地在 `dispatch_motor_task()` 发布命令前把电机加入 `active_motors`，发布失败时移除，防止“创建消息到达前”重复下发。

### 3. 设备离线时清零所有电机数据
- `_mark_device_offline()` 中把该设备所有电机的 `rpm`、`health_status`、`zero_samples` 清零，并同步到数据库，避免离线后仍显示旧转速。

### 4. 多电机健康状态使用 `active_motors`
- `_update_motor_health()` 之前只根据 `current_task.motor` 判断，多电机时会被后一个电机覆盖。现在改为检查该电机是否在 `active_motors` 集合中，支持多电机同时显示 `Running`。

## 关于 `task_finished` 的确认
已确认 ESP32-S3 固件会发送 `task_finished`：
- 文件：`esp32_idf/main/pid.c`
- 格式：`task_finished_<motor_index>_<speed>_<duration>`
- Topic：`esp32/<mac>/task`
- 后端订阅了 `esp32/+/+`，因此能正确收到。

在 `main/` 目录中没有发现其他名称的“任务完成”特征码，目前只有 `task_create_...` 和 `task_finished_...` 两种。

## 验证
- `python -m py_compile main_page/mqtt.py main_page/scheduler.py main_page/views.py`：通过。
- `python manage.py check`：通过。
- `npm test -- --run`：50 个测试全部通过。

Let me write the logic.

computeRefreshInterval:
```js
computeRefreshInterval() {
    const task = this.currentTask
    if (!task || !task.started_at || !task.expected_finished_at) return 5000
    const now = Date.now()
    const start = new Date(task.started_at).getTime()
    const end = new Date(task.expected_finished_at).getTime()
    const durationSec = (end - start) / 1000
    if (durationSec < 15) return 100  // 10Hz
    const elapsedSec = (now - start) / 1000
    const remainingSec = (end - now) / 1000
    if (elapsedSec <= 5 || remainingSec <= 5) return 100  // 10Hz
    return 1000  // 1Hz
}
```

But current_task is only for one motor. For multi-motor, the last motor's times are used. That's acceptable.

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Looks good. On finished, active_motors discard, if empty set task_status idle and current_task {}, and clear telemetry rpm/health_status.

But wait: `_update_device_telemetry` updates telemetry and syncs to DB. `_update_device_task` also syncs to DB but only task_status/current_task, not telemetry. So when we clear rpm in _update_device_task, the DB telemetry isn't updated. But `get_motors` reads from in-memory state via `get_device_states()`, not DB. So it's fine for the HTTP API. However, `device_list` reads telemetry from state too. Good.

But there's a subtle issue: `_update_device_task` only syncs DB with task_status/current_task. It doesn't sync telemetry. The telemetry clearing is only in memory. But that's fine because get_device_states returns in-memory state.

Actually, `_update_motor_health` syncs telemetry to DB. But _update_device_task doesn't. So DB might have stale telemetry, but API uses in-memory. That's OK for now.

Now, the real potential bug: after task_finished clears telemetry, if a late pcnt_rpm message arrives (with rpm non-zero), `_update_motor_health` will check `motor in active_motors`. Since we removed it, is_target_motor=False, so health_status='idle'. But `_update_device_telemetry` still sets rpm to the received value. So Actual RPM would show the late non-zero value even though status is idle.

To fix this, in `_update_device_telemetry` or `_update_motor_health`, if motor is not in active_motors, we should not update rpm? Or set rpm=0? Actually, we could ignore rpm updates for motors not in active_motors. But that might miss legitimate data.

Better: in `_update_device_telemetry`, if motor not in active_motors and key=='rpm', set value=0? No, that's wrong if motor is genuinely running without active_motors (e.g. after restart).

Actually, the real fix is to set rpm=0 in `_update_device_task` finished, AND in `_update_motor_health`, if motor not active, set rpm=0. But _update_motor_health doesn't set rpm, it only reads it.

Wait, `_update_device_telemetry` sets rpm. Then `_update_motor_health` reads it. So if a late message sets rpm=100, health_status=idle, Actual RPM=100. We don't want that.

We could modify `_update_device_telemetry` to not update rpm if motor not in active_motors. But then how would idle rpm be updated? The firmware sends rpm=0 when motor stops. If we ignore it, rpm stays at last value.

Actually, the cleanest is: in `_update_device_telemetry`, always update the value. In `_update_motor_health`, if motor not active, set `telemetry[motor_key]['rpm'] = 0` and health_status='idle'. This ensures non-active motors show rpm=0.

But what about a motor that's running but active_motors wasn't updated? That shouldn't happen if task_create is received.

Actually, there's another case: after backend restart, active_motors is empty. But device might be running a task. Then rpm updates would be set to 0 incorrectly. But that's a transient state.

For the user's case, the main issue is late messages after task_finished. Setting rpm=0 for non-active motors is the right fix.

Let me update `_update_motor_health` to set rpm=0 when motor not active.

</details>

## 本次改动

### 1. 修复 `status` 一直显示 `Running` 且 `Actual RPM` 不归零
**根因**：上次修改中，`_handle_device_event` 处理 `task_finished_...` 时注释里写着“调用 `_update_device_task`”，但实际代码漏掉了该调用。导致：
- `active_motors` 没有被移除；
- `current_task` 没有清空；
- 电机 `RPM` 和 `health_status` 没有被清零。

因此电机 `Availability` 虽然通过 `can_dispatch_motor` 恢复了 `Available`，但前端拿到的 `status` 和 `Actual RPM` 仍是旧值。

**修复**：
- 重新加上 `_update_device_task(effective_device_id, motor, speed, duration, 'finished')`。
- `_update_device_task('finished')` 会：
  - 从 `active_motors` 移除该电机；
  - 所有电机都空闲后将设备恢复为 `idle`；
  - 将该电机的 `rpm` 清零、`health_status` 置 `idle`。
- `_update_motor_health()` 对非 active 电机收到转速消息时，也把 `rpm` 清零，避免任务结束后的延迟消息让 `Actual RPM` 重新变回旧值。

### 2. 动态刷新频率
在 `Spinning.vue` 中实现电机状态动态轮询：

| 场景 | 刷新频率 |
|------|---------|
| 无运行中任务 | 5 秒（保持原频率） |
| 任务总时长 < 15 秒 | 10Hz（100ms）全程 |
| 任务开始后前 5 秒 | 10Hz |
| 任务结束前前 5 秒 | 10Hz |
| 任务中间时段 | 1Hz |

实现细节：
- `get_motors/` 接口现在返回 `current_task` 和 `active_motors`，前端据此计算当前应使用的轮询间隔。
- 使用动态 `setTimeout` 递归调度，根据每次返回的 `current_task` 自动切换间隔。
- 预约记录列表仍保持 5 秒刷新。
- 手动刷新按钮依然立即触发 `getMotors()`，不受当前间隔限制。

## 问题根因
`_auto_release_stale_busy_state()` 只在超时时把设备 `task_status` 恢复为 `idle`、清空 `active_motors`，但没有清零电机的 `rpm` 和 `health_status`。因此虽然 `Availability` 恢复为 `Available`，前端拿到的 `status` 仍然是 `Running`，`Actual RPM` 也保持旧值。

## 修复
更新 `django_backend/main_page/mqtt.py` 中的 `_auto_release_stale_busy_state()`：

- 自动释放 stale busy 状态时，同步将该设备所有电机的：
  - `rpm` 清零
  - `health_status` 置为 `idle`
  - `zero_samples` 清零
- 并将更新后的 `telemetry` 写回数据库，保持内存与数据库一致。

现在无论是收到 `task_finished` 还是触发 auto-release，电机状态都会正确恢复为 `Idle` 且 `Actual RPM` 归零。

- (motor_name in running_motors OR motor_names overlap with running_motors)

But for simplicity, we can just update all SENT/RUNNING records for the device to FINISHED. Since auto-release means all running motors on the device are stale, all related records should be finished.

Wait, but there could be records that are SENT but the motor isn't running? No, SENT means dispatched. If device is busy, those are the running tasks. So updating all SENT/RUNNING records for the device to FINISHED is safe.

Actually, what if multiple tasks were dispatched to different motors and some finished normally while others didn't? The normally finished ones would already be FINISHED. Only the stuck ones remain SENT/RUNNING. So updating all remaining SENT/RUNNING to FINISHED is correct.

Let me implement this.

</details>

已修复并提交 git。

## 修复内容
当设备 `busy` 状态因超时触发 `auto-release` 时，现在会同步查找并更新关联的 `Spinning` 记录：

- 匹配条件：
  - `device_id` 相同
  - 状态为 `SENT` 或 `RUNNING`
  - `motor_name` 或 `motor_names` 与当前正在运行的电机匹配
- 更新为：
  - `status = 'FINISHED'`
  - `finished_at = now()`
  - `error_message = 'Finished by auto_release: task_finished not received within timeout'`
  - `updated_at = now()`

这样 `Registration List` 中因未收到 `task_finished` 而卡住的任务也会正确结束，不再一直显示 `RUNNING`/`SENT`。

- `python -m py_compile main_page/mqtt.py`：通过。
- `python manage.py check`：通过。
- `npm test -- --run`：50 个测试全部通过。
- `npm run build`：构建成功。

     1	# ESP32S3 MQTT 连接长时间监控功能实现记录
     2	
     3	**日期**: 2026-02-25  
     4	**分支**: `feature/mqtt-connection-monitor`  
     5	**任务描述**: 实现 MQTT 连接长时间监控与统计功能，用于统计特定时间（如四小时）内的断连次数，分析 WiFi 信号偏弱 (-72dBm) 导致的偶发包丢失问题
     6	
     7	---
     8	
     9	## 1. 背景
    10	
    11	### 历史问题回顾
    12	根据 2026-02-10 的修复记录，ESP32S3 存在 MQTT 连接不稳定问题：
    13	- 断开间隔不规律（10-180秒）
    14	- 错误类型：`No PING_RESP`、`Connection reset by peer`
    15	- 已实施的修复（禁用 WiFi 省电模式、优化 MQTT 配置）收效有限
    16	- 推测原因：WiFi 信号偏弱 (-72dBm) 导致偶发包丢失
    17	
    18	### 本次任务目标
    19	创建专门的监控分支，实现长时间监控功能，统计4小时内的断连次数，为后续分析提供数据支撑。
    20	
    21	---
    22	
    23	## 2. 监控功能设计
    24	
    25	### 2.1 需求分析
    26	
    27	| 需求项 | 说明 |
    28	|--------|------|
    29	| 记录内容 | 每次连接/断开事件的时间戳、断开原因 |
    30	| 统计维度 | 总运行时间、连接次数、断开次数、连接保持率 |
    31	| 报告频率 | 每4小时输出一次完整统计报告 |
    32	| 存储限制 | 最近100次断开事件，循环覆盖 |
    33	| **时间同步** | **通过 SNTP 同步实际时间，确保记录准确** |
    34	
    35	### 2.2 数据结构
    36	
    37	```c
    38	// 单次断开事件记录
    39	typedef struct {
    40	    int64_t disconnect_time_ms;     // 断开时间戳
    41	    int64_t reconnect_time_ms;      // 重连时间戳
    42	    int64_t disconnect_duration_ms; // 断开持续时长
    43	    const char* disconnect_reason;  // 断开原因
    44	} disconnect_event_t;
    45	
    46	// MQTT连接统计数据结构体
    47	typedef struct {
    48	    // 时间同步相关（新增）
    49	    bool time_synced;               // 时间是否已同步
    50	    int64_t boot_time_ms;           // 开机时的系统时间（毫秒）
    51	    time_t boot_real_time;          // 开机时的实际时间（UTC时间戳）
    52	    int64_t time_sync_time_ms;      // 时间同步完成时的系统时间
    53	    
    54	    // 连接统计
    55	    int total_connections;          // 总连接次数
    56	    int total_disconnects;          // 总断开次数
    57	    int64_t first_connect_time_ms;  // 首次连接时间
    58	    int64_t current_session_start_ms; // 当前连接会话开始时间
    59	    int64_t total_connected_time_ms; // 累计连接时长
    60	    
    61	    // 断开事件日志（循环缓冲区）
    62	    disconnect_event_t disconnect_log[100];
    63	    int log_index;                  // 当前日志索引
    64	    int log_count;                  // 有效日志数量
    65	    
    66	    // 当前状态
    67	    bool is_connected;              // 当前连接状态
    68	    int64_t last_disconnect_time_ms; // 上次断开时间
    69	} mqtt_connection_stats_t;
    70	```
    71	
    72	---
    73	
    74	## 3. 实现方案
    75	
    76	### 3.1 新增文件
    77	
    78	#### `main/monitor.h`
    79	- 监控模块头文件
    80	- 定义数据结构体和配置参数
    81	- 声明监控函数接口
    82	- **新增 SNTP 配置：**
    83	  - `NTP_SERVER_PRIMARY`: `cn.pool.ntp.org`（国内NTP服务器）
    84	  - `NTP_SERVER_BACKUP`: `ntp.aliyun.com`（阿里云NTP服务器）
    85	
    86	#### `main/monitor.c`
    87	- 监控模块实现
    88	- 事件记录函数：`monitor_record_connect()` / `monitor_record_disconnect()`
    89	- 统计报告函数：`monitor_report_statistics()`
    90	- 监控任务：`monitor_task()` (每4小时输出报告)
    91	- **新增 SNTP 同步功能：**
    92	  - `monitor_start_time_sync()`: 启动 SNTP 同步
    93	  - `monitor_wait_time_sync()`: 等待同步完成
    94	  - `monitor_is_time_synced()`: 检查同步状态
    95	  - `monitor_get_current_time_str()`: 获取实际时间字符串
    96	  - `monitor_get_elapsed_time_str()`: 获取运行时长
    97	- **新增互斥锁保护：**
    98	  - `stats_mutex`: 保护共享变量的互斥锁
    99	  - `set_time_synced()` / `get_time_synced()`: 安全访问同步状态
   100	
   101	### 3.2 修改文件
   102	
   103	#### `main/main.c`
   104	```c
   105	// 创建MQTT连接监控任务
   106	xTaskCreate(monitor_task, "MONITOR_TASK", 4096, NULL, 2, NULL);
   107	```
   108	
   109	#### `main/mqtt.c`
   110	在 MQTT 事件处理回调中添加监控调用：
   111	- `MQTT_EVENT_CONNECTED`: 调用 `monitor_record_connect()`
   112	- `MQTT_EVENT_DISCONNECTED`: 调用 `monitor_record_disconnect("No PING_RESP / Connection reset")`
   113	- `MQTT_EVENT_ERROR`: 调用 `monitor_record_disconnect("MQTT_EVENT_ERROR")`
   114	
   115	#### `main/wifi.c`（新增 SNTP 启动）
   116	```c
   117	else if (event_base == IP_EVENT && event_id == IP_EVENT_STA_GOT_IP)
   118	{
   119	    ip_event_got_ip_t *event = (ip_event_got_ip_t *)event_data;
   120	    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Got ip:" IPSTR, IP2STR(&event->ip_info.ip));


</details>

   124	class SpinningScheduler:
   125	    @classmethod
   126	    def _dispatch_task(cls, task):
   127	        claimed = Spinning.objects.filter(id=task.id, status='PENDING').update(status='SENDING')
   128	        if claimed == 0: return
   129	        task = Spinning.objects.get(id=task.id)
   130	        motor = Motor.objects.filter(name=task.motor_name).first()

   134	            Spinning.objects.filter(id=task.id).update(status='SENT', dispatched_at=now)

   136	            Spinning.objects.filter(id=task.id).update(status='FAILED', error_message=str(exc))

<system>120 lines read from file starting from line 1. Total lines in file: 460.</system>
     1	# ESP32S3 电机控制策略演进记录：从 PID 闭环到开环死区补偿
     2	
     3	**日期**: 2026-07-10  
     4	**分支**: `main`  
     5	**任务描述**: 梳理并记录从基于代码的 PID 调节、发现电机内置速度闭环、决定采用开环控制、验证开环有效性，到最终加入死区补偿的完整思考与修改过程
     6	
     7	---
     8	
     9	## 1. 背景
    10	
    11	### 1.1 系统概述
    12	
    13	项目使用 ESP32-S3-DevKitC-1 控制 4 路 CHB-BLDC2418 无刷直流电机，核心控制链路为：
    14	
    15	```
    16	MQTT 命令 → control_cmd 任务 → motor_speed_list[index]
    17	                                    ↓
    18	PID 任务(pid.c) ← pcnt_count_list[index] ← PCNT 采样(pcnt.c)
    19	                                    ↓
    20	                            PWM 输出(pwm.c) → 电机
    21	```
    22	
    23	- **PWM**: GPIO 1/4/6/8，5KHz，13-bit（0~8191），反相逻辑（8191=OFF，0=ON）
    24	- **PCNT**: GPIO 2/5/7/9，200ms 采样周期（5Hz），乘以 5 转换为每秒脉冲数
    25	- **FG 信号**: 6 pulses/rotation
    26	- **电机**: CHB-BLDC2418，12V 供电，实测空载约 4500 RPM
    27	
    28	### 1.2 历史问题
    29	
    30	截至 2026-07-08，代码存在以下主要问题：
    31	
    32	| # | 问题 | 现象 |
    33	|---|------|------|
    34	| 1 | PID 结构错误：位置式与增量式混合 | `output = data->pre_input + output` 导致积分/历史输出被累加两次，振荡与超调 |
    35	| 2 | 微分项对设定值突变敏感 | `derivative = (error - data->pre_error)` 产生 Derivative Kick |
    36	| 3 | 积分限幅不精确 | 用 `±max_pwm` 限幅 `integral`，Ki 很小时过于保守 |
    37	| 4 | 量程混淆 | `PID_MAX_PCNT` 一度被改为 900（对应 9000 RPM，24V 规格），但 12V 实际空载仅 4500 RPM |
    38	| 5 | 软启动与状态重置耦合混乱 | `pre_input` 残留影响下一次启动 |
    39	| 6 | PCNT 异常阈值过低 | 150/200ms 在高速时可能误判 |
    40	
    41	---
    42	
    43	## 2. Phase 1: PID 结构修复与量程修正
    44	
    45	### 2.1 诊断摘要
    46	
    47	基于 `2026_07_moter_modify_3/prompt/2026_07_10.md` 对 `main/pid.c`、`main/pcnt.c`、`main/pwm.c` 进行审查，确认上述 6 个问题及代码位置。
    48	
    49	### 2.2 关键决策
    50	
    51	| 决策项 | 结果 | 说明 |
    52	|--------|------|------|
    53	| 电机规格 | 12V / 4500 RPM（空载实测） | 9000 RPM 为 24V 规格，当前 12V 供电不适用 |
    54	| `PID_MAX_PCNT` | 改回 450（pulses/sec） | 对应 4500 RPM（12V 实测） |
    55	| `PID_data` 结构体 | 最小改动 | 保留 `pre_input`/`d_filtered`，新增 `pre_output`，复用 `pre_measurement` |
    56	| `PID_Calculate` | 纯位置式 + 微分先行 + 条件积分 | 解决积分累加两次与 Derivative Kick 问题 |
    57	| Rate Limiter | 软启动前 2s 每周期最大增加 300；正常运行每周期最大变化 450 | 替代原有绝对上限软启动 |
    58	
    59	### 2.3 修改文件
    60	
    61	- `main/main.h`：新增 `PID_terms` 分项输出结构体；`PID_data` 新增 `pre_output`；更新 `PID_Calculate` 声明
    62	- `main/pid.c`：重写 `PID_Calculate`；`PID_init` 改为 Rate Limiter 软启动；启动/停止边沿清零状态；新增 `pid_log_terms()` 解耦日志
    63	- `main/pcnt.c`：异常阈值 150 → 250；突刺检测 `5x+30` → `8x+50`
    64	- `hardware_info/CHB-BLDC2418-Motor-Configuration.md`：更新为 12V/4500 RPM 实测规格
    65	- `AGENTS.md`：同步电机规格与 Max PCNT 描述
    66	
    67	### 2.4 关键代码
    68	
    69	```c
    70	// 位置式 PID + 微分先行（Derivative on Measurement）+ 条件积分
    71	double PID_Calculate(struct PID_params params, struct PID_data *data, 
    72	                     double target_speed, double current_speed, struct PID_terms *terms)
    73	{
    74	    double error = target_speed - current_speed;
    75	    double Pout = params.Kp * error;
    76	    
    77	    // 微分先行：对测量值微分，避免设定值突变导致的 Derivative Kick
    78	    double Dout = params.Kd * (data->pre_measurement - current_speed);
    79	    
    80	    // 条件积分：预测输出是否会朝饱和方向加剧
    81	    double predicted_output = Pout + params.Ki * (data->integral + error) + Dout;
    82	    bool saturate_high = (predicted_output > params.max_pwm && error > 0);
    83	    bool saturate_low  = (predicted_output < params.min_pwm && error < 0);
    84	    
    85	    if (!saturate_high && !saturate_low) {
    86	        data->integral += error;
    87	        double integral_max = params.max_pwm / params.Ki;
    88	        if (data->integral > integral_max) data->integral = integral_max;
    89	        if (data->integral < -integral_max) data->integral = -integral_max;
    90	    }
    91	    
    92	    double Iout = params.Ki * data->integral;
    93	    double output = Pout + Iout + Dout;  // 纯位置式，不再与历史输出累加
    94	    
    95	    if (output > params.max_pwm) output = params.max_pwm;
    96	    else if (output < params.min_pwm) output = params.min_pwm;
    97	    
    98	    data->pre_error = error;
    99	    data->pre_measurement = current_speed;
   100	    
   101	    if (terms != NULL) {
   102	        terms->Pout = Pout;
   103	        terms->Iout = Iout;
   104	        terms->Dout = Dout;
   105	        terms->error = error;
   106	    }
   107	    
   108	    return output;
   109	}
   110	```

```json
  "content": "# Multi-Device Spinning Dashboard and Motor State Management Fixes\n\n**日期**: 2026-07-16  \n**分支**: `main`  \n**任务描述**: 改造 `Spinning.vue` 控制台以支持多台 ESP32S3 设备（按 MAC 区分）的电机状态分页显示与任务注册；修复任务下发后设备/电机状态卡死、Actual RPM 不归零、WebSocket 序列化失败等问题；实现任务运行期间的动态刷新频率。\n\n---\n\n## 1. 背景\n\n### 1.1 系统概述\n\n项目使用 ESP32-S3-DevKitC-1 控制 4 路 CHB-BLDC2418 无刷直流电机，Django 后端通过 MQTT 与设备通信，Vue 前端提供 `Spinning.vue` 操作界面：\n\n```\nSpinning.vue (Vue 3)\n    ├── Motor Status Board   (电机实时状态)\n    ├── Register Spin Task   (预约/立即下发任务)\n    └── Registration List    (任务历史与状态)\n```\n\n- **控制命令**: `cmd_<motor_index>_<speed_rpm>_<duration_sec>`，例如 `cmd_2_800_10`\n- **任务事件**: `task_create_<motor>_<speed>_<duration>`、`task_finished_<motor>_<speed>_<duration>`\n- **状态缓存**: 后端使用内存 `_device_states` 维护设备在线/任务/遥测状态\n- **心跳/遥测**: `esp32/<mac>/heartbeat`、`esp32/<mac>/telemetry`\n\n### 1.2 历史问题\n\n截至 2026-07-16 之前，代码存在以下主要问题：\n\n| # | 问题 | 现象 |\n|---|------|------|\n| 1 | 单设备视角 | `Motor Status Board` 只显示自动解析出的单台设备电机，无法区分多台 ESP32S3 |\n| 2 | 设备级 Availability | 任一台电机 busy 即整台设备所有电机显示 `Unavailable` |\n| 3 | 任务完成状态死锁 | 未收到 `task_finished` 时设备永远 `busy`，必须重启后端 |\n| 4 | 状态不重置 | 任务结束后 `status` 仍显示 `Running`，`Actual RPM` 不归零 |\n| 5 | WebSocket 崩溃 | `_device_states` 中的 `set` 类型无法 JSON 序列化，导致 WebSocket 连接断开 |\n| 6 | 多电机调度失败 | 调度器向同一设备连续下发多个电机时，第二个电机被 `can_dispatch` 拦截 |\n| 7 | 错误提示不统一 | 使用原生 DOM 块显示错误，未使用 Element Plus Message |\n| 8 | 刷新不及时 | 固定 5 秒轮询，无法及时观察电机启动/停止瞬态 |\n\n---\n\n## 2. 需求确认\n\n通过与用户多轮确认，最终确定以下需求：\n\n| 需求项 | 说明 |\n|--------|------|\n| 多设备支持 | `Motor Status Board` 按 Device 分页，标签显示 `esp32_<mac>` 格式 |\n| 电机级可用性 | 一台设备上部分电机运行时，其他空闲电机仍显示 `Available` |\n| 多电机调度 | `Register Spin Task` 支持选择同一 Device 的多个电机，单条 `Spinning` 记录存储电机列表 |\n| 手动刷新 | 点击刷新立即获取当前选中 Device 的电机状态 |\n| 动态刷新 | 无任务时 5 秒；任务运行时按阶段使用 10Hz/1Hz/10Hz |\n| 状态恢复 | 任务结束（收到 `task_finished` 或超时释放）后，`status`、`Actual RPM` 恢复 |\n| 错误提示 | 统一使用 `ElMessage.error` / `ElMessage.success` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. 实现方案\n\n### 3.1 新增/修改文件\n\n#### 后端\n\n| 文件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| `django_backend/main_page/models.py` | `Spinning` 新增 `motor_names` JSONField，兼容旧版 `motor_name` |\n| `django_backend/main_page/serializer.py` | `SpinningSerializer` 允许 `motor_name` 可选、`motor_names` 可写 |\n| `django_backend/main_page/migrations/0034_spinning_motor_names.py` | 新增字段迁移 |\n| `django_backend/main_page/views.py` | `get_motors` 支持 `device_id`；`spinning` 支持 `device_id` + `motor_names` |\n| `django_backend/main_page/scheduler.py` | 多电机循环下发；首个电机校验，后续直接发布 |\n| `django_backend/main_page/mqtt.py` | 电机级可用性、active_motors 跟踪、状态自动释放、遥测清零、WebSocket 序列化兼容 |\n\n#### 前端\n\n| 文件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| `vue_frontend/src/services/api/motors.js` | `getList` 支持 `device_id`；`createSchedule` 支持新 payload |\n| `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/MotorStatusBoard.vue` | Device 分页标签 + 手动刷新按钮 + 设备摘要 |\n| `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleForm.vue` | Device 选择 + 多电机复选框 + 电机索引排序 |\n| `vue_frontend/src/components/spinning/ScheduleQueue.vue` | 显示 Device 与 Motors 列 |\n| `vue_frontend/src/views/Dashboard/Spinning.vue` | 设备选择、状态缓存、动态轮询、ElMessage 错误提示 |\n| `vue_frontend/src/__tests__/api/motors.spec.js` | 更新 API 测试 |\n\n#### 文档\n\n| 文件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| `2026_06_to_07_develop_detail/07_16/plan/2026_07_16_multi_device_spinning_dashboard_plan.md` | 实施计划副本 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Phase 1: Multi-Device Dashboard Foundation\n\n### 4.1 数据模型调整\n\n```python\nclass Spinning(models.Model):\n    motor_name = models.CharField(max_length=128, null=False)\n    motor_names = models.JSONField(default=list, blank=True)\n    device_id = models.CharField(max_length=32, default='esp32_1')\n    ...\n\n    def effective_motor_names(self):\n        if self.motor_names:\n            return list(self.motor_names)\n        return [self.motor_name] if self.motor_name else []\n```\n\n### 4.2 后端 API 调整\n\n- `POST /api/get_motors/` 增加可选 `device_id` 参数，返回指定设备的 4 路电机状态。\n- `POST /api/spinning/` 创建任务时接收 `device_id` + `motor_names`，校验电机存在性。\n- 列表接口补充 `mac_address`、`device_label`、`motor_display`。\n\n### 4.3 前端重构\n\n- `Spinning.vue` 新增 `devices`、`selectedDeviceId`、`deviceMotors` 状态。\n- `MotorStatusBoard.vue` 渲染 Excel 式底部标签页，MAC 格式化为 `esp32_7cdfa1e6d3cc`。\n- `ScheduleForm.vue` 增加 Device 下拉框与 4 路电机多选。\n- `ScheduleQueue.vue` 增加 Device/Motors 列。\n\n---\n\n## 5. Phase 2: Per-Motor Availability and State Lifecycle\n\n### 5.1 电机级可用性\n\n新增 `can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index)`：\n\n```python\ndef can_dispatch_motor(device_id, motor_index):\n    ...\n    if task_status == 'busy':\n        active_motors = state.get('active_motors', set())\n        if motor_index in active_motors:\n            return False, f'Motor {motor_index} is busy'\n    return True, ''\n```\n\n- `get_motors()` 对每个电机单独判断 `avaliable`。\n- `dispatch_motor_task()` 使用电机级校验。\n- `resolve_dispatchable_device_id()` 支持传入 `motor_index`。\n\n### 5.2 active_motors 跟踪\n\n设备状态增加 `active_motors: set()`：\n\n- `task_create`：加入集合。\n- `task_finished`：移除集合；为空时恢复 `idle`。\n- 下发命令前乐观加入，发布失败时移除。\n- 急停/确认/离线时清空。\n\n### 5.3 状态自动释放\n\n新增 `_auto_release_stale_busy_state(device_id, grace_seconds=5)`：\n\n- 当 `current_task.expected_finished_at + grace_seconds` 过后仍未收到 `task_finished`，自动释放。\n- 清零所有电机 `rpm`、`health_status`、`zero_samples`。\n- 将仍处于 `SENT`/`RUNNING` 的 `Spinning` 记录置为 `FINISHED`。\n- 用户已将宽限期调整为 **5 秒**。\n\n---\n\n## 6. Phase 3: Bug Fixes and Polish\n\n### 6.1 WebSocket 序列化\n\n`get_device_states()` 返回前将 `active_motors` 从 `set` 转换为排序后的 `list`：\n\n```python\nif isinstance(copy.get('active_motors'), set):\n    copy['active_motors'] = sorted(copy['active_motors'])\n```\n\n### 6.2 Actual RPM 与 status 不归零\n\n- 修复 `_handle_device_event` 中 `task_finished` 分支漏掉 `_update_device_task('finished')` 的回归。\n- `_update_device_task('finished')` 中清零该电机 `rpm`、`health_status`。\n- `_update_motor_health()` 对非 active 电机收到转速时强制 `rpm = 0`。\n- `_mark_device_offline()` 清零所有电机遥测。\n\n### 6.3 多电机连续下发\n\n调度器首个电机校验设备可用，后续电机 `check_dispatch=False` 直接发布：\n\n```python\nfor index, motor in enumerate(motors):\n    result = dispatch_motor_task(\n        device_id, motor.motor_index, task.motor_speed, task.duration_sec,\n        check_dispatch=(index == 0)\n    )\n```\n\n### 6.4 动态刷新频率\n\n`Spinning.vue` 根据 `current_task` 动态计算轮询间隔：\n\n| 条件 | 间隔 |\n|------|------|\n| 无任务 | 5000 ms |\n| 总时长 < 15 s | 100 ms |\n| 开始后 5 s 内 | 100 ms |\n| 结束前 5 s 内 | 100 ms |\n| 中间时段 | 1000 ms |\n\n使用递归 `setTimeout` 而非固定 `setInterval`。\n\n### 6.5 错误提示与标签格式\n\n- 移除 `ScheduleForm.vue` 的 `console-message` 块，统一使用 `ElMessage.error`。\n- Device 标签统一为 `esp32_<mac>`，不再显示带冒号的 MAC。\n- 选中电机按索引从小到大排序。\n\n---\n\n## 7. 测试与验证\n\n| 检查项 | 命令 | 结果 |\n|--------|------|------|\n| Django 系统检查 | `python manage.py check` | 通过 |\n| Python 语法检查 | `python -m py_compile ...` | 通过 |\n| 前端单元测试 | `npm test -- --run` | 50/50 通过 |\n| 前端生产构建 | `npm run build` | 成功 |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Git 提交记录\n\n```text\n0c1979a  feat(Spinning): task lifecycle states, past-time guard, live motor status board, remove QuickControl\n        (前期已完成的基础工作，来自上下文压缩)\nd640e11  feat(spinning): multi-device motor status board and task registration\nd2bb919  fix(spinning): device label format, motor sorting, availability bug, ElMessage errors\n97d368c  fix(mqtt): WebSocket serialization, multi-motor dispatch, stale busy state\n57030dd  fix(mqtt): per-motor availability, clear RPM on finish/offline\nc32fd12  fix(spinning): task finish state reset and dynamic motor polling\nb664f3c  fix(mqtt): clear telemetry on stale busy auto-release\n7e3bf09  fix(mqtt): finish stale Spinning records on auto-release\n```\n\n> 所有提交均排除 `django_backend/db.sqlite3`。\n\n---\n\n## 9. 附：同日的 esp32_serial_logger.py 修复\n\n在 2026-07-16 上午的会话中，还针对 `e:\\Platform_G2\\esp32_serial_logger.py` 进行了排查：\n\n- 问题：驱动重装后（Silicon Labs CP210x，COM9）无串口输出。\n- 排查：文件位于 `e:\\Platform_G2\\esp32_serial_logger.py`；环境缺少 `pyserial`。\n- 结论：需确认端口描述符/波特率/编码是否与驱动变更后一致；若后续需要正式修复，将另开计划文档。\n\n---\n\n## 10. 备注\n\n- `2026_06_to_07_develop_detail/07_16/log/` 下的会话导出仅用于汇总，未提交 git。\n- 当前 `grace_seconds` 已设置为 **5 秒**。\n- 后续如继续扩展多设备能力，可考虑：\n  - 在 `Spinning.vue` 中为每个 Device 缓存独立的 `current_task` 以支持多设备同时高频率刷新。\n  - 为 `Registration List` 增加设备筛选或分页。\n"
```
